function v36CheckSpanish(){
 const d=V35_SPANISH[v35.index],inp=document.getElementById('v36Entry');v35.input=inp.value;
 if(!v35.input.trim()){document.getElementById('v36Message').textContent='You can type a guess, or click “Show me how.”';return;}

}
// Full one-octave keyboard, large enough on a phone. No sideways scrolling and no hidden notes.
v35RealKeyboard=function(){
 const white=['C4','D4','E4','F4','G4','A4','B4','C5'], black={C:'C#',D:'D#',F:'F#',G:'G#',A:'A#'};
 return `<div class="v36-keyboard" role="group" aria-label="One-octave playable piano keyboard, C4 through C5"><div class="v36-keyboard-hint">LOWER NOTES <span>Higher notes →</span></div><div class="v35-real-keyboard v36-one-octave" style="--v35-white-count:8">${white.map(n=>`<button type="button" class="v35-white-key" data-v34-key="${n}" aria-label="Play ${n}"><span>${n==='C4'?'C4':n.replace(/[45]/,'')}</span></button>`).join('')}${white.map((n,i)=>black[n[0]]&&i<7?`<button type="button" class="v35-black-key" data-v34-key="${black[n[0]]+n.slice(1)}" aria-label="Play ${black[n[0]]+n.slice(1)}" style="left:${(i+1)*100/8}%"><span>${black[n[0]]}</span></button>`:'').join('')}</div><p class="v36-keys-guide"><b>Find C:</b> Look for the group of two black keys. C is the white key immediately to their left. Tap any key to hear it.</p></div>`;
};
v35Piano=function(){
 const d=V35_PIANO[v35.index],done=v35.step>=d.notes.length,next=d.notes[v35.step];
 v35Frame(`${v36Top('piano',V35_PIANO)}<article class="v36-lesson"><span class="v36-kicker">PIANO · NO MUSIC KNOWLEDGE NEEDED</span><h1>${v35h(d.title)}</h1><p class="v36-hook">${v35h(V36_PIANO_SIMPLE[v35.index]||d.why)}</p><section class="v36-practice v36-piano-practice"><div class="v36-step">1 <span>Do this on the keyboard</span></div><h2>${v35h(d.prompt)}</h2><p class="v36-gentle">Finger suggestion: ${v35h(d.finger)}</p><div class="v36-sequence" aria-label="Notes in this exercise">${d.notes.map((n,i)=>`<span class="${i<v35.step?'done':i===v35.step?'now':''}">${v35h(n)}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="v36-note-heading">${done?'<b>You played it!</b>':`<span>Next note: </span><strong id="v36Target">${v35.independent?'Find it without the highlight':v35h(next)}</strong>`}</div>${v35RealKeyboard()}<div id="v35PianoFeedback" class="v36-message" role="status">${done?'You completed the phrase. Play it once more from memory if you like.':'Tap the highlighted key. A wrong note is just a chance to hear the difference.'}</div><div class="v36-piano-controls"><button id="v36HearNote">▶ Hear the next note</button><button id="v36ResetPiano">↺ Play from the beginning</button><label><input id="v36Memory" type="checkbox" ${v35.independent?'checked':''}/> Hide the highlight</label></div></section><div class="v36-outcome" ${done?'':'hidden'} id="v36PianoDone"><h2>That is a real skill learned.</h2><p>Try it without highlighted notes when you're comfortable. You do not need speed, sheet music, or perfection to move forward.</p><button class="v36-primary" id="v36PianoNext">${v35.index===V35_PIANO.length-1?'Go back to lesson one':'Next piano lesson →'}</button></div>${v36Next('piano',V35_PIANO)}</article>`);
 v36TopWire('piano');v36NextWire('piano',V35_PIANO);
 const paint=()=>{document.querySelectorAll('.v36-keyboard [data-v34-key]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('v35-target',!v35.independent&&b.dataset.v34Key===d.notes[v35.step]));const t=document.getElementById('v36Target');if(t)t.textContent=v35.independent?'Find it by memory':d.notes[v35.step]};paint();
 document.querySelectorAll('.v36-keyboard [data-v34-key]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v35PianoPress(b.dataset.v34Key));
 document.getElementById('v36HearNote').onclick=()=>{const n=d.notes[v35.step]||d.notes[0];v35.help=true;v27Sound(n);document.getElementById('v35PianoFeedback').textContent='That sound is '+n+'. Find the glowing key and try it yourself.'};
 document.getElementById('v36ResetPiano').onclick=()=>{v35.step=0;v35.errors=0;v35.help=false;v35Piano()};
 document.getElementById('v36Memory').onchange=e=>{v35.independent=e.target.checked;paint();document.getElementById('v35PianoFeedback').textContent=v35.independent?'The highlight is hidden. You can turn it on whenever you want.':'The next note is glowing again.'};
 const n=document.getElementById('v36PianoNext');if(n)n.onclick=()=>v35Open('piano',(v35.index+1)%V35_PIANO.length);
};
v35PianoPress=function(n){
 const d=V35_PIANO[v35.index];v27Sound(n);if(v35.step>=d.notes.length)return;
 const expected=d.notes[v35.step],fb=document.getElementById('v35PianoFeedback');
 if(n!==expected){v35.errors++;fb.textContent='You played '+n+'. Hear the difference, then find '+expected+'. The next key is highlighted.';return;}
 v35.step++;
 if(v35.step>=d.notes.length){v35Save('piano:'+v35.index,v35.independent&&!v35.help&&!v35.errors);v35Piano();return;}
 const seq=document.querySelectorAll('.v36-sequence span');seq.forEach((el,i)=>el.className=i<v35.step?'done':i===v35.step?'now':'');
 document.getElementById('v36Target').textContent=v35.independent?'Find it by memory':d.notes[v35.step];
 document.querySelectorAll('.v36-keyboard [data-v34-key]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('v35-target',!v35.independent&&b.dataset.v34Key===d.notes[v35.step]));
 fb.textContent='Yes, that was '+n+'. Keep going — just one key at a time.';
};

<script>
/* V41 — Spanish timeline and why each tense exists, from right now to unreal possibilities. */
const V41_SPANISH_STAGES=[
 ['NOW','Present','Hablo español.','Facts, habits, what is true now.',[0,1,2,3,5]],
 ['NEXT','Plans & future','Voy a hablar.','Plans: ir a + infinitive. Predictions: future endings.',[4,10,11]],
 ['PAST','Finished past','Ayer hablé.','Events that happened and ended.',[6,7,8,9]],
 ['BACKGROUND','Imperfect','Cuando era niño, jugaba.','What used to happen; what was ongoing.',[12,13]],
 ['MAYBE','Conditional','Hablaría si pudiera.','What would happen under a condition.',[14,15]],
 ['POSSIBLE','Subjunctive','Espero que vengas.','Wishes, emotions, doubts and uncertain outcomes.',[16,17,18,19]]
];

<script>
/* V42 — Short conjugation gym: verb + person + why this ending. */
const V42_FORMS=[
 ['hablar','speak','present','yo','hablo','Remove -ar and add -o for I.'],
 ['hablar','speak','present','tú','hablas','Remove -ar and add -as for you (informal).'],
 ['hablar','speak','present','nosotros','hablamos','Remove -ar and add -amos for we.'],
 ['comer','eat','present','yo','como','Remove -er and add -o for I.'],
 ['comer','eat','present','tú','comes','Remove -er and add -es for you.'],
 ['vivir','live','present','yo','vivo','Remove -ir and add -o for I.'],
 ['vivir','live','present','nosotros','vivimos','Remove -ir and add -imos for we.'],
 ['ser','be (identity)','present','yo','soy','Ser is irregular: learn soy as one useful unit.'],
 ['ser','be (identity)','present','ellos','son','Ser is irregular: son means they are.'],
 ['estar','be (state)','present','yo','estoy','Estar is irregular: estoy describes my state or location.'],
 ['estar','be (state)','present','tú','estás','Estás is the informal you-form of estar.'],
 ['tener','have','present','yo','tengo','The yo-form of tener is tengo.'],
 ['querer','want','present','yo','quiero','Querer changes e → ie in this form.'],
 ['poder','can','present','yo','puedo','Poder changes o → ue in this form.'],
 ['ir','go','present','yo','voy','Ir is irregular: voy means I go/am going.'],
 ['hablar','speak','preterite','yo','hablé','For finished -ar events, yo ends in -é.'],
 ['hablar','speak','preterite','él','habló','For finished -ar events, él/ella ends in -ó.'],
 ['comer','eat','preterite','yo','comí','For finished -er/-ir events, yo ends in -í.'],
 ['vivir','live','preterite','nosotros','vivimos','Nosotros -ir forms look identical in present and preterite; context tells them apart.'],
 ['hablar','speak','imperfect','yo','hablaba','Imperfect -ar verbs: -aba. Habitual/background past.'],
 ['comer','eat','imperfect','yo','comía','Imperfect -er/-ir verbs: -ía. Habitual/background past.'],
 ['hablar','speak','future','yo','hablaré','Keep the infinitive and add -é for future yo.'],
 ['comer','eat','future','nosotros','comeremos','Infinitive + -emos for future nosotros.'],
 ['hablar','speak','conditional','yo','hablaría','Infinitive + -ía for would speak.']
];
let v42Index=0,v42Correct=0;
function v42Gym(i=0){v42Index=(i+V42_FORMS.length)%V42_FORMS.length;v35.section='spanish';const d=V42_FORMS[v42Index];v35Frame(`<section class="v50-gym"><span class="v36-kicker">SPANISH · CORE VERBS</span><div class="v50-gym-top"><h1>One useful verb at a time</h1><span>${v42Index+1} / ${V42_FORMS.length}</span></div><p>You're practicing what real speakers use constantly, not random vocabulary.</p><div class="v50-gym-card"><span>Say this form</span><h2>${d[3]} + ${d[0]}</h2><p>${d[1]} · ${d[2]} tense</p><label for="v42Input">Your answer</label><input id="v42Input" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="Type the Spanish verb form"/><div class="v50-gym-actions"><button id="v42Check">Check</button><button id="v42Show">Show & explain</button></div><div id="v42Feedback" class="v50-gym-feedback" role="status"></div></div><div class="v50-gym-actions"><button id="v42Prev">← Previous</button><button id="v42Next">Next verb →</button><button id="v42Back">Return to Spanish lessons</button></div></section>`);
 const fb=document.getElementById('v42Feedback');let attempts=0;
 const assess=(show=false)=>{const raw=document.getElementById('v42Input').value.trim();if(!raw&&!show){fb.textContent='Try the form, or choose Show & explain.';return;}const correct=!show&&v35N(raw)===v35N(d[4]);if(!correct&&!show)attempts++;
 fb.innerHTML=`<strong>${correct?'Exactly!':show?'Model answer':'Not quite — learn the pattern'} ${v35h(d[4])}</strong><p>${v35h(d[5])}</p>`;
 if(correct&&!attempts){v42Correct++;v35Save('verbform:'+v42Index,true)}else if(correct)v35Save('verbform:'+v42Index,false);
 };
 document.getElementById('v42Check').onclick=()=>assess();document.getElementById('v42Show').onclick=()=>assess(true);
 document.getElementById('v42Input').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')assess()};
 document.getElementById('v42Prev').onclick=()=>v42Gym(v42Index-1);document.getElementById('v42Next').onclick=()=>v42Gym(v42Index+1);document.getElementById('v42Back').onclick=()=>v35Open('spanish',v36Resume('spanish'));
}
const v42SpanishPrevious=v35Spanish;
v35Spanish=function(){v42SpanishPrevious();if(v35.mode!=='grammar')return;const el=document.querySelector('.v36-lesson');if(!el)return;const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='v50-inline-link';b.textContent='Practice essential verb endings →';b.onclick=()=>v42Gym();el.querySelector('.v36-explainer')?.append(b);};

</script>
<script>
/* V43 — Tap-to-build a full Spanish sentence, then see why the pieces work. */
function v43Tokens(text){return text.replace(/[¿?¡!.,;:]/g,'').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)}
let v43Selection=[];
function v43Builder(index=v35.index){const d=V35_SPANISH[index];if(!d)return;v35.section='spanish';v43Selection=[];
 const parts=v43Tokens(d.es);const order=parts.map((_,i)=>i).sort((a,b)=>(a*17+7)%parts.length-(b*17+7)%parts.length);
 v35Frame(`<div class="v50-builder"><span class="v36-kicker">BUILD IT · LESSON ${index+1}</span><h1>Build a sentence you can actually say</h1><p>Tap the Spanish words in the right order. Start with the meaning; the grammar explanation comes afterward.</p><div class="v50-builder-english">${v35h(d.en)}</div><div id="v43Selection" class="v50-builder-selection" aria-live="polite"></div><div id="v43Tiles" class="v50-tiles">${order.map(i=>`<button data-v43-token="${i}">${v35h(parts[i])}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="v50-gym-actions"><button id="v43Undo">Undo last</button><button id="v43Reveal">Show sentence + why</button><button id="v43Return">Return to lesson</button></div><div id="v43Feedback" class="v50-gym-feedback" role="status"></div></div>`);
 const chosen=document.getElementById('v43Selection'),feedback=document.getElementById('v43Feedback');const paint=()=>{chosen.innerHTML=v43Selection.length?v43Selection.map(i=>'<span>'+v35h(parts[i])+'</span>').join(' '):'<em>Your sentence will appear here…</em>';
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v43-token]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=v43Selection.includes(Number(b.dataset.v43Token)));
 if(v43Selection.length===parts.length){const answer=v43Selection.map(i=>parts[i]).join(' ');const match=v35N(answer)===v35N(d.es);feedback.innerHTML=`<strong>${match?'You built it!':'Close — here is the natural order:'} ${v35h(d.es)}</strong><p>${v35h(d.explanation)}</p><p>${v35h(d.why)}</p>`;v35Save('phrase:'+index,match);}};
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v43-token]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{v43Selection.push(Number(b.dataset.v43Token));paint()});
 document.getElementById('v43Undo').onclick=()=>{v43Selection.pop();feedback.textContent='';paint()};
 document.getElementById('v43Reveal').onclick=()=>{feedback.innerHTML=`<strong>${v35h(d.es)}</strong><p>${v35h(d.explanation)}</p><p>${v35h(d.why)}</p>`};
 document.getElementById('v43Return').onclick=()=>v35Open('spanish',index);paint();
}

 {name:'Why do fevers change how you feel?',story:'During an infection, a person experiences chills as body temperature begins to rise.',question:'What explains the chills?',options:['The hypothalamic temperature set point has risen','Muscles have stopped generating heat','The body has lost all immune cells'],answer:0,mechanism:'Inflammatory signals can raise the hypothalamic temperature set point. <script>
/* V46 — Coordinate/geometry sandbox; distinct from full legal chess engine in archive. */
const V46_PIECES=[['knight','♘','e4','Knights jump in an L: two squares along a rank/file and one perpendicular.'],['rook','♖','d4','Rooks travel any number of squares horizontally or vertically.'],['bishop','♗','c4','Bishops travel diagonally; their square color never changes.'],['queen','♕','d4','Queens combine rook and bishop movement.'],['king','♔','e4','Kings move one square in any direction. Kings cannot enter check in a real game.'],['pawn','♙','e2','White pawns move forward toward rank 8. From their initial rank they can move two squares if clear; they capture diagonally.']];
let v46PieceIndex=0,v46Selected='';
function v46Sandbox(i=0){v46PieceIndex=(i+V46_PIECES.length)%V46_PIECES.length;v46Selected='';v35.section='chess';const [name,glyph,from,rule]=V46_PIECES[v46PieceIndex],files='abcdefgh',f=files.indexOf(from[0]),r=Number(from[1]);
 const legal=(file,rank)=>{const x=Math.abs(file-f),y=Math.abs(rank-r);if(file===f&&rank===r)return false;if(name==='knight')return(x===1&&y===2)||(x===2&&y===1);if(name==='rook')return x===0||y===0;if(name==='bishop')return x===y;if(name==='queen')return x===0||y===0||x===y;if(name==='king')return Math.max(x,y)===1;return file===f&&(rank===r+1||(rank===r+2&&r===2));};
 v35Frame(`<div class="v50-chess-sandbox"><span class="v36-kicker">CHESS · SEE HOW EACH PIECE MOVES</span><h1>Tap a square. See what happens.</h1><p>${v35h(rule)}</p><div class="v50-chess-chooser">${V46_PIECES.map(([n],j)=>`<button data-v46-piece="${j}" ${j===v46PieceIndex?'aria-pressed="true"':''}>${n}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="v50-chess-grid" role="group" aria-label="Chess movement practice board">${Array.from({length:64},(_,i)=>{let row=Math.floor(i/8),col=i%8,rank=8-row,coordinate=files[col]+rank,isPiece=coordinate===from,move=legal(col,rank);return `<button aria-label="${coordinate}${move?' legal destination':''}" data-v46-target="${coordinate}" data-v46-can="${move?'1':'0'}" class="${(row+col)%2?'dark':'light'} ${move?'move':''} ${isPiece?'piece':''}">${isPiece?glyph:(move?'<span class="v50-chess-dot"></span>':'')}<small>${col===0?rank:''}${row===7?files[col]:''}</small></button>`}).join('')}</div><div class="v50-chess-message" id="v46Feedback" role="status">The piece starts on ${from}. Choose a highlighted square to try its movement.</div><div class="v50-gym-actions"><button id="v46Next">Try another piece →</button><button id="v46Return">Back to chess lessons</button></div><p class="v36-smallprint">Movement practice only. Checks, occupied squares, castling and full chess legality belong in the playable chess course.</p></div>`);
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v46-piece]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v46Sandbox(Number(b.dataset.v46Piece)));
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v46-target]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const good=b.dataset.v46Can==='1',dest=b.dataset.v46Target;document.getElementById('v46Feedback').textContent=good?`Yes — ${name} can move from ${from} to ${dest} on an otherwise empty board.`:`Not that square. ${rule}`;document.querySelectorAll('.v50-chess-grid button').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('v50-chess-selected',x===b));if(good)v35Save('movement:'+name,true);});
 document.getElementById('v46Next').onclick=()=>v46Sandbox(v46PieceIndex+1);document.getElementById('v46Return').onclick=()=>v35Open('chess',v36Resume('chess'));
}
const v46ModulePrevious=v35Module;
v35Module=function(section,arr){v46ModulePrevious(section,arr);if(section!=='chess')return;const ex=document.querySelector('.v36-explainer');if(!ex)return;const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='v50-inline-link';b.textContent='Practice piece movement on a board →';b.onclick=()=>v46Sandbox();ex.append(b);};

</script>

const V47_CHECKS=[

<script>
/* V49 — Review what you know. Counts actual attempts; no pressure to maintain streaks. */
const V49_SECTIONS=['spanish','piano','medicine','chess','books'];
function v49ReviewItems(){const p=v35Progress(),now=Date.now(),day=86400000,out=[];
 for(const sec of V49_SECTIONS){for(let i=0;i<v36Length(sec);i++){const key=sec+':'+i,info=p[key];if(!info?.seen)continue;
 const last=Date.parse(info.date||'')||0,days=(now-last)/day,spacing=info.mastered?7:1;
 out.push({section:sec,index:i,title:(sec==='books'?V35_BOOKS:sec==='spanish'?V35_SPANISH:sec==='piano'?V35_PIANO:sec==='medicine'?V35_MEDICINE:V35_CHESS)[i].title,mastered:!!info.mastered,due:days>=spacing,days:Math.floor(Math.max(0,days))});}}
 return out.sort((a,b)=>Number(b.due)-Number(a.due)||Number(a.mastered)-Number(b.mastered)||b.days-a.days);
}
function v49Review(){v35.section='home';const all=v49ReviewItems(),due=all.filter(x=>x.due),practiced=all.filter(x=>!x.mastered),shown=[...new Map([...due,...practiced,...all].map(x=>[x.section+':'+x.index,x])).values()].slice(0,12);
 v35Frame(`<section class="v50-review"><span class="v36-kicker">REMEMBER IT FOR REAL</span><h1>What should I revisit?</h1><p>Learning feels easier when yesterday's ideas start coming back automatically. Start with something you explored with help, then return to older material. Nothing is overdue in a punitive sense.</p><div class="v50-review-stats"><span><strong>${all.length}</strong> lessons explored</span><span><strong>${due.length}</strong> ready for another look</span><span><strong>${practiced.length}</strong> worth another independent try</span></div>${shown.length?`<div class="v50-review-list">${shown.map(x=>`<button data-v49-review="${x.section}:${x.index}"><span>${v35h(V36_SECTION_NAMES[x.section])} · ${x.mastered?'Previously solved':'Try without help'}</span><strong>${v35h(x.title)}</strong><small>${x.due?'Time to revisit':x.mastered?'Remembered recently':'Build confidence'}</small></button>`).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="v50-review-empty"><h2>Nothing to review yet.</h2><p>Complete a single guided lesson. Afterward, the review room will find it for you.</p></div>'}<div class="v50-gym-actions"><button id="v49Home">Back to subjects</button></div></section>`);
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v49-review]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const [s,i]=b.dataset.v49Review.split(':');v35Open(s,Number(i))});document.getElementById('v49Home').onclick=()=>v35Open('home');
}
const v49HomePrevious=v35Home;
v35Home=function(){v49HomePrevious();const host=document.querySelector('.v36-home-hero');if(!host)return;const note=document.createElement('div');note.className='v50-review-entry';const all=v49ReviewItems(),due=all.filter(x=>x.due).length,weak=all.filter(x=>!x.mastered).length;note.innerHTML=`<button id="v49OpenReview">↻ Review what you've learned <span>${due?`${due} ready for review`:weak?`${weak} to practice independently`:'At your own pace'} →</span></button>`;host.append(note);document.getElementById('v49OpenReview').onclick=v49Review;};

</script>
<script>
/* V50 — Kind first wins, precise help, responsive clarity and progress backup. */
const V50_STARTERS={
 piano:{title:'Find your first note',idea:'The white key immediately left of two black keys is C. On our small keyboard, that is middle C (C4).',question:'What should you look for to find C?',options:['A group of two black keys','The very last key on every piano','The tallest white key'],correct:0,why:'A pair of black keys is a reliable landmark.  spanish:{title:'Say something useful',idea:'The ending on a Spanish verb often tells you who is doing it.  medicine:{title:'The cell needs energy',idea:'Imagine a membrane pump lifting something uphill.  chess:{title:'Find the white queen',idea:'White starts at the bottom. Files are a–h from left to right; ranks are 1–8 going away from White.  books:{title:'Keep one great idea',idea:'You don’t have to memorize a book. You need one idea you can put to work.  document.getElementById('v50FullLesson').onclick=()=>v35Open(section,v36Resume(section));document.getElementById('v50StarterHome').onclick=()=>v35Open('home');

function v50Help(){const s=v35.section,d=s==='spanish'?V35_SPANISH[v35.index]:s==='medicine'?V35_MEDICINE[v35.index]:s==='chess'?V35_CHESS[v35.index]:s==='piano'?V35_PIANO[v35.index]:null;
 if(!d)return;
 const note=s==='spanish'?`Start with the English meaning: ${d.en}. function v51Plot(points,lines=[]){const W=440,H=250,left=42,right=18,top=13,bottom=32;const xa=x=>left+(x/120)*(W-left-right), ya=y=>H-bottom-(y/120)*(H-bottom-top);

function v51Lab(track='Microeconomics'){
 const header=`<div class="v51-actions"><button id="v51BackMap">← Economics roadmap</button>${V51_TRACKS.map(t=>`<button data-v51-lab="${t}" ${t===track?'class="v51-primary"':''}>${t}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
 let content='';if(track==='Microeconomics')content=`<h1>Move demand and supply. Watch a market find its price.</h1><p>Demand slopes down: higher prices mean fewer buyers. Supply slopes up: higher prices attract more sellers. Equilibrium is where both agree.</p><div class="v51-controls"><label>How strong is demand? <b id="v51DemandV">100</b><input id="v51Demand" type="range" min="60" max="150" value="100"></label><label>How much supply? <b id="v51SupplyV">20</b><input id="v51Supply" type="range" min="0" max="50" value="20"></label></div><div id="v51Chart"></div><p class="v51-result" id="v51Answer"></p><p class="v51-note">Experiment: increase demand, then increase supply. Which raises equilibrium price? Why?</p>`;
 else if(track==='Macroeconomics')content=`<h1>GDP: separate real growth from higher prices.</h1><p>Nominal GDP can rise because goods become more expensive, even if the economy produces exactly the same quantity.</p><div class="v51-controls"><label>New price per unit <b id="v51PriceV">12</b><input id="v51Price" type="range" min="5" max="22" value="12"></label><label>New number of units <b id="v51QtyV">105</b><input id="v51Qty" type="range" min="70" max="140" value="105"></label></div><div id="v51Chart"></div><p class="v51-result" id="v51Answer"></p><p class="v51-note">Reference year: 100 units at $10 each. This is a simple single-good demonstration, not full national-income accounting.</p>`;
 else content=`<h1>Regressions can be changed by one unusual observation.</h1><p>A regression line minimizes squared errors. Change the final observation and see how the estimated slope moves. Fit is not the same as causation.</p><div class="v51-controls"><label>Last point’s outcome <b id="v51OutlierV">10</b><input id="v51Outlier" type="range" min="0" max="30" value="10"></label></div><div id="v51Chart"></div><p class="v51-result" id="v51Answer"></p><p class="v51-note">The observations are deliberately invented. A slope measures fitted association, not necessarily what would happen if you changed X.</p>`;
 v35Frame(`<section class="v51-econ v51-lab"><span class="v36-kicker">INTERACTIVE EXPERIMENT · ${track}</span>${header}${content}<div class="v51-actions"><button id="v51ToLessons" class="v51-primary">Try the lessons →</button></div></section>`);
 document.getElementById('v51BackMap').onclick=v51Map;document.getElementById('v51ToLessons').onclick=()=>v51TrackStart(track);document.querySelectorAll('[data-v51-lab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v51Lab(b.dataset.v51Lab));
 const render=()=>{if(track==='Microeconomics'){const a=+document.getElementById('v51Demand').value,c=+document.getElementById('v51Supply').value,b=2,d=2,p=(a-c)/(b+d),q=a-b*p;document.getElementById('v51DemandV').textContent=a;document.getElementById('v51SupplyV').textContent=c;document.getElementById('v51Chart').innerHTML=v51Plot([[q,p,'#d97706']],[{points:[[0,a/b],[a,0]],color:'#2d71a8'},{points:[[c,0],[120,(120-c)/d]],color:'#419665'}]);document.getElementById('v51Answer').textContent='Equilibrium price: $'+p.toFixed(2)+' · quantity: '+q.toFixed(1)+' units. Demand ↑ tends to raise price; supply ↑ tends to lower it.';}
 else if(track==='Macroeconomics'){const price=+document.getElementById('v51Price').value,qty=+document.getElementById('v51Qty').value,nom=(price*qty)/1000*100-100,real=qty-100,infl=(price/10-1)*100;document.getElementById('v51PriceV').textContent=price;document.getElementById('v51QtyV').textContent=qty;document.getElementById('v51Chart').innerHTML=v51Plot([[100,100,'#28754e'],[Math.min(qty,120),Math.min(price*qty/10,120),'#c78228']]);document.getElementById('v51Answer').textContent='Nominal GDP growth: '+nom.toFixed(1)+'% · real GDP growth: '+real.toFixed(1)+'% · price change: '+infl.toFixed(1)+'%.';}
 else {const y=+document.getElementById('v51Outlier').value,pts=[[1,2],[2,4],[3,6],[4,8],[5,y]],mx=3,my=(20+y)/5,cov=pts.reduce((a,[x,z])=>a+(x-mx)*(z-my),0),denom=pts.reduce((a,[x])=>a+(x-mx)**2,0),slope=cov/denom,inter=my-slope*mx;document.getElementById('v51OutlierV').textContent=y;document.getElementById('v51Chart').innerHTML=v51Plot(pts.map(([x,z])=>[x*20,z*3]),[{points:[[20,(inter+slope)*3],[100,(inter+5*slope)*3]],color:'#2d71a8'}]);document.getElementById('v51Answer').textContent='Fitted slope: '+slope.toFixed(2)+' · intercept: '+inter.toFixed(2)+'. The line minimizes squared residuals.';}};
 ['v51Demand','v51Supply','v51Price','v51Qty','v51Outlier'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('input',render));render();}

<script>

/* V52: opt-in reading color scheme, adapted from Google's V38; no unsafe note HTML. */
(function(){
 'use strict';
 const KEY='studytable_v52_night';
 let night=false;try{const saved=localStorage.getItem(KEY);night=saved===null?!!window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches:saved==='1'}catch{}
 function apply(){
  if(window.studyTableTheme){window.studyTableTheme.sync();return}
  const portal=document.getElementById('v35Portal');if(!portal)return;
  portal.classList.toggle('v52-night',night);
  const btn=portal.querySelector('.v52-mode');
  if(btn){btn.textContent=night?'Day reading':'Night reading';btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(night));}
 }
 function mount(){
  const head=document.querySelector('#v35Portal .v36-head-actions');
  if(!head){apply();return;}
  if(!head.querySelector('.v52-mode')){
   const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='v52-mode';
   btn.onclick=()=>{if(window.studyTableTheme){window.studyTableTheme.set(window.studyTableTheme.get()==='dark'?'light':'dark');return}night=!night;try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,night?'1':'0')}catch{};apply()};
   head.prepend(btn);
  }
  apply();
 }
 // The portal is created lazily, so attach to the view renderer rather than looking for it only at page load.
 const previousFrame=v35Frame;
 v35Frame=function(...args){const result=previousFrame(...args);mount();return result;};

})();

<script>
/* V53: real effort, lower interface friction. Preserves all archive and earlier curricula. */
(function(){
 'use strict';
 const k='studytable_v53_prepared';
 // Deterministically distribute correct options, without changing their meaning.
 const rotateQuestions=(list)=>list.forEach((item,i)=>{
   if(!item||!Array.isArray(item.options)||item.options.length<2||item._v53rotated)return;
   const n=item.options.length, right=item.options[item.answer];
   // Deliberately cover positions A, B, C instead of repeatedly choosing A.
   const target=(i+1)%n, diff=(target-item.answer+n)%n;
   const options=Array.from({length:n},(_,j)=>item.options[(j-diff+n)%n]);
   item.options=options;item.answer=target;item._v53rotated=true;
   if(item.options[item.answer]!==right)throw Error('Answer distribution changed solution');
 });
 rotateQuestions(V35_MEDICINE);rotateQuestions(V35_CHESS);
 Object.keys(V50_STARTERS).forEach((subject,i)=>{
   const d=V50_STARTERS[subject];if(!d||d._v53rotated)return;
   const n=d.options.length,target=(i+2)%n,diff=(target-d.correct+n)%n,original=d.options[d.correct];
   d.options=Array.from({length:n},(_,j)=>d.options[(j-diff+n)%n]);d.correct=target;d._v53rotated=true;
   if(original!==d.options[target])throw Error('Starter choice reordering failed');
 });
 const escape=s=>v35h(String(s));

       const current=v35.index,section=v35.section;
       group.addEventListener('click',event=>{
         const button=event.target.closest('[data-v36-choice]');if(!button||button.disabled||v35.index!==current||v35.section!==section)return;
         if(Number(button.dataset.v36Choice)===(section==='medicine'?V35_MEDICINE:V35_CHESS)[current].answer)return;

           event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();event.stopImmediatePropagation();

           button.classList.add('v53-almost');

             ?'Try once more: what mechanism actually makes this happen? Read the explanation above, then compare the remaining choices.'
             :'Try once more: check the square, piece movement, or immediate threat. Look at the explanation above, then choose again.';

<script>
/* V54 — The patient teacher. Contextual guidance, explicit limits, teach-back practice. */
(function(){
 'use strict';
 const enc=x=>v35h(String(x??''));
 const moment={
  'present':'now, a repeated habit, or something generally true',
  'progressive':'an action happening right now',
  'near future':'a plan you are going to carry out',
  'preterite':'a finished event in the past',
  'imperfect':'a past habit, description, or background scene',
  'present perfect':'past experience connected to now',
  'future':'a prediction, promise, or future event',
  'conditional':'what would happen under a condition',
  'subjunctive':'a wish, doubt, feeling, or imagined possibility',
  'imperative':'a request or direction to someone',
  'pluperfect':'something that had already happened before another past event'
 };
 function whenText(d){const phase=String(d.phase||'').toLowerCase();
  const keys=['pluperfect','present perfect','near future','preterite','imperfect','progressive','conditional','subjunctive','imperative','future','present'];
  return (keys.find(k=>phase.includes(k))||'present') in moment ? moment[keys.find(k=>phase.includes(k))||'present'] : 'the situation described in the lesson';
 }
 function questionFor(section,index){
  if(section==='spanish') return 'When would you use this form, and what changes in the verb?';
  if(section==='medicine') return 'What is the mechanism connecting the cause to the effect?';
  if(section==='chess') return 'What would you check before choosing a move?';
  if(section==='economics') return 'What changes in this situation, and why?';
  if(section==='piano')return 'Can you find the starting note without the highlight?';
  return 'What is the one idea you could explain to someone else?';
 }
 function answerFor(section,index){const d=section==='spanish'?V35_SPANISH[index]:section==='medicine'?V35_MEDICINE[index]:section==='chess'?V35_CHESS[index]:section==='economics'?V51_ECON[index]:null;
  if(!d)return 'Play the same notes once more, this time with the highlight off.';
  if(section==='spanish')return 'Use it for '+whenText(d)+'. '+d.form+' Example: '+d.es;
  if(section==='medicine'||section==='chess')return (d.why||d.core||'')+(d.action?' '+d.action:'');
  return (d.why||'')+' '+(d.pitfall||'');
 }
 function markHelp(){if(typeof v35==='object'&&v35) v35.help=true;}
 function injectCoach(portal,section,index){
  const isSpanish=section==='spanish',isPiano=section==='piano',isEcon=section==='economics';
  const d=isSpanish?V35_SPANISH[index]:section==='medicine'?V35_MEDICINE[index]:section==='chess'?V35_CHESS[index]:isEcon?V51_ECON[index]:isPiano?V35_PIANO[index]:null;
  if(!d)return;
  const task=portal.querySelector(isEcon?'#v51Question':'.v36-practice');if(!task)return;
  const helper=document.createElement('details');helper.className='v54-help';helper.dataset.v54Help=section;
  helper.innerHTML='<summary><span aria-hidden="true">?</span> I’m stuck — help me understand</summary><div class="v54-help-inner"><p class="v54-support">No need to guess blindly. Unfold the explanation one step at a time.</p><div class="v54-help-content" aria-live="polite"></div><div class="v54-help-actions"><button type="button" data-v54-stage="1">1 · What am I looking for?</button><button type="button" data-v54-stage="2">2 · Give me the rule</button><button type="button" data-v54-stage="3">3 · Walk me through it</button></div></div>';
  if(isPiano){const keyboard=task.querySelector('.v36-keyboard');if(keyboard)keyboard.after(helper);else task.append(helper)}
  else {const choices=task.querySelector(isEcon?'.v51-options':'.v36-choice-list')||task.querySelector('.v36-question');if(choices)choices.after(helper);else task.append(helper)}
  let unlocked=0;const container=helper.querySelector('.v54-help-content');
  const getStage=s=>{
    if(isSpanish){
      if(s===1)return 'This sentence expresses '+whenText(d)+'. Ask: who is acting, and when?';
      if(s===2)return 'Verb to build from: '+d.verb+'. '+d.form;
      return 'Read the situation: '+d.en+' The model makes sense because '+d.explanation+' You can now try writing your own sentence or use “Show me how.”';
    }
    if(isPiano){
      if(s===1)return 'Focus on ONE note, not the whole keyboard. The next target is '+(d.notes?.[v35.step]||d.notes?.[0]||'the highlighted note')+'.';
      if(s===2)return 'C is the white key immediately to the left of a pair of black keys. D is between that pair; E is to their right. The note names then continue F, G, A, B.';
      return 'Tap “Hear this note” to listen, then press the highlighted key yourself. A wrong key is fine: listen to the difference and try again.';
    }
    if(section==='medicine'){
      if(s===1)return 'The question is asking: '+d.question+' Before choosing, identify the cause and the body process it changes.';
      if(s===2)return d.core||d.why;
      return 'Read the real case: '+(d.case||d.question)+' Ask which option best matches the mechanism, not which uses the most medical words.';
    }
    if(section==='chess'){
      if(s===1)return 'Start with the question: '+d.question+' Look for what is attacked, undefended, or forced.';
      if(s===2)return d.core||d.why;
      return 'Use this over-the-board test: '+(d.action||'First check checks, then captures, then threats. Before moving, inspect the opponent’s reply.');
    }
    if(s===1)return 'Think about the concrete situation: '+d.example;
    if(s===2)return 'The governing idea: '+d.idea+' '+d.formula;
    return 'Before deciding, watch for this trap: '+d.pitfall+' Then compare your options.';
  };
  helper.querySelectorAll('[data-v54-stage]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    const target=Number(btn.dataset.v54Stage);if(target>unlocked+1)return;
    markHelp();unlocked=Math.max(unlocked,target);const steps=[];
    for(let i=1;i<=unlocked;i++)steps.push('<p class="v54-hint-step"><b>'+i+'.</b> '+enc(getStage(i))+'</p>');
    container.innerHTML=steps.join('');helper.querySelectorAll('[data-v54-stage]').forEach(b=>{
      const n=Number(b.dataset.v54Stage);b.disabled=n>unlocked+1;b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(n<=unlocked));
    });
  }));
  helper.querySelectorAll('[data-v54-stage]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=Number(b.dataset.v54Stage)>1);
 }
 function addTeachback(portal,section,index){
  const after=portal.querySelector('#v36Outcome')||portal.querySelector('#v51Feedback')||(section==='piano'?portal.querySelector('.v36-piano-practice'):null);
  if(!after)return;
  const teach=document.createElement('details');teach.className='v54-teachback';teach.innerHTML='<summary>One more minute: explain it back (optional)</summary><div class="v54-teachback-body"><p>'+enc(questionFor(section,index))+'</p><label>In your own words<textarea rows="3" placeholder="One or two sentences is enough…"></textarea></label><div class="v54-teach-actions"><button type="button" class="v54-primary" data-v54-save>Save what I understood</button><button type="button" data-v54-example>Compare with a model explanation</button></div><p data-v54-message role="status" aria-live="polite"></p><div data-v54-model hidden class="v54-model"><b>Example explanation (not an automatic grade)</b><p>'+enc(answerFor(section,index))+'</p></div></div>';
  after.after(teach);
  const textarea=teach.querySelector('textarea'),msg=teach.querySelector('[data-v54-message]'),model=teach.querySelector('[data-v54-model]');
  const key=section+':'+index;
  try{textarea.value=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('studytable_v54_teachback')||'{}')[key]?.text||''}catch{}
  teach.querySelector('[data-v54-save]').onclick=()=>{
    if(!textarea.value.trim()){msg.textContent='Write a sentence first, even if it is imperfect.';return}
    try{const data=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('studytable_v54_teachback')||'{}');data[key]={text:textarea.value.trim(),savedAt:new Date().toISOString()};localStorage.setItem('studytable_v54_teachback',JSON.stringify(data));msg.textContent='Saved here on this device. This is practice, not an automatic correctness score.'}
    catch{msg.textContent='Browser storage is unavailable. Copy your explanation if you want to keep it.'}
  };
  teach.querySelector('[data-v54-example]').onclick=()=>{markHelp();model.hidden=false;msg.textContent='Compare the reasoning, not just the words. Try rewriting your own explanation afterward.'};
 }
 function enhanceRetries(portal,section,index){
  if(!['medicine','chess'].includes(section))return;
  const container=portal.querySelector('.v36-choice-list');if(!container)return;
  const d=(section==='medicine'?V35_MEDICINE:V35_CHESS)[index];const feedback=portal.querySelector('#v53Retry');if(!feedback)return;
  const parent=portal.querySelector('.v36-practice')||container;
  parent.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const target=e.target.closest('[data-v36-choice]');if(!target)return;
    if(Number(target.dataset.v36Choice)===d.answer)return;
    setTimeout(()=>{
      if(v35.index!==index||v35.section!==section||feedback.hidden)return;
      feedback.textContent='You picked “'+target.textContent.trim().replace(/^[ABC]\s*/, '')+'.” Check the key idea: '+(d.core||d.why)+' Then choose again. The full worked explanation comes after your next attempt.';
    });
  },true);
 }
 function enhanceSpanish(portal,index){
  const practice=portal.querySelector('.v36-practice');if(!practice)return;
  const d=V35_SPANISH[index];
  const nav=document.createElement('aside');nav.className='v54-time-lens';
  nav.innerHTML='<b>What time are we talking about?</b><p>'+enc(whenText(d))+'.</p><small>Rule to try: '+enc(d.form)+'</small>';
  const q=practice.querySelector('.v36-question');if(q)q.before(nav);
 }
 function enhanceHome(portal){
  const hero=portal.querySelector('.v36-home-hero');if(!hero)return;
  const p=v35Progress();const guided=['spanish','piano','medicine','chess','economics'];const unfinished=[];
  for(const section of guided){const n=v36Length(section);for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const st=p[section+':'+i];if(st?.seen&&!st?.mastered){unfinished.push({section,i});break}}}
  if(!unfinished.length)return;
  const {section,i}=unfinished[0];const note=document.createElement('div');note.className='v54-review-invitation';
  note.innerHTML='<div><strong>One idea you explored earlier</strong><p>No pressure to repeat it. If you want, try it again without the hints.</p></div><button type="button">Practice independently →</button>';
  hero.append(note);note.querySelector('button').onclick=()=>v35Open(section,i);
 }
 const oldCheckSpanish=v36CheckSpanish;
 v36CheckSpanish=function(...args){
   const result=oldCheckSpanish(...args);const portal=document.getElementById('v35Portal');
   const d=V35_SPANISH[v35.index],status=portal?.querySelector('#v36Outcome');
   const input=portal?.querySelector('#v36Entry')?.value.trim();
   if(!d||!status||status.hidden||!input)return result;
   status.querySelector('.v54-sentence-tip')?.remove();
   const tip=document.createElement('p');tip.className='v54-sentence-tip';
   const n=s=>v35N(s).replace(/^yo\s+/,'');
   tip.textContent=n(input)===n(d.es)?'Your sentence matches the model. To remember it, say it once without looking.':'Your sentence differs from the authored model, but that does not automatically make it wrong. Compare tense, speaker and meaning. For truly new sentences, an online tutor or Spanish speaker can evaluate alternatives.';
   status.insertBefore(tip,status.querySelector('.v36-outcome-actions'));
   return result;
 };
 const oldSave=v35Save;
 v35Save=function(key,independent=false){if(v35 && v35.help && key===v35.section+':'+v35.index)independent=false;return oldSave(key,independent);};
 const previousFrame=v35Frame;
 v35Frame=function(markup){
  previousFrame(markup);
  const portal=document.getElementById('v35Portal');if(!portal)return;
  const section=v35?.section||'home',index=v35?.index||0;
  if(section==='home')enhanceHome(portal);
  if(['piano','spanish','medicine','chess','economics'].includes(section)){

   if(['medicine','chess'].includes(section))enhanceRetries(portal,section,index);
   addTeachback(portal,section,index);
  }
 };
 const prevInit=v35Init;v35Init=function(){prevInit();const title=document.querySelector('header .brand small');if(title)title.textContent='V54 · LEARN WITH CLARITY';};
})();

<script>
/* Study Table V55 — From confusion to a smaller solvable step. */
(function(){
 'use strict';
 const escape = x => v35h(String(x ?? ''));
 const FOUNDATION = {
   spanish: {
    0: ['Present means a habit or something true now. “I speak” is hablo.','Which describes a habit?',['I speak Spanish every day.','I spoke Spanish yesterday.'],0,'“Every day” signals a habit, so use present: hablo.'],
    1: ['Ser identifies what someone is or where they are from. Estar tells where someone is or how they feel.','Which verb expresses where you are from?',['estar','ser'],1,'Origin takes ser: Soy de Nueva York. Being tired today takes estar: estoy cansado.'],
    2: ['You do not need every verb ending today. Learn useful whole phrases first: tengo = I have; quiero = I want; puedo = I can.','How do you say “I want”?',['quiero','tengo'],0,'Quiero expresses wanting; tengo expresses having.'],
    3: ['A simple Spanish negative goes before the conjugated verb: No entiendo. A question can use the same order with question intonation.','How do you say “I do not understand”?',['Entiendo no.','No entiendo.'],1,'Put no directly before entiendo.'],
    4: ['For a near-future plan use voy a plus an infinitive. Infinitive means the unchanged verb: estudiar, comer, hablar.','Which means “I am going to study”?',['Voy a estudiar.','Estudio ayer.'],0,'Voy a estudiar describes an intended plan.'],
    5: ['The progressive is for something unfolding right now. Use estoy plus a form ending in -ando or -iendo.','Which means “I am eating right now”?',['Comí ahora.','Estoy comiendo ahora.'],1,'Estoy comiendo puts the action in progress.'],
    6: ['A finished event at a definite past time normally uses the preterite. Hablar → hablé for “I spoke.”','Which goes with “yesterday”?',['Ayer hablé.','Ayer hablo.'],0,'Ayer hablé: a completed event yesterday.'],
    7: ['Many high-frequency past verbs are irregular: fui = I went; hice = I did/made; vi = I saw. Learn them in mini-stories.','Which means “I went”?',['hice','fui'],1,'Fui is the preterite of ir for “I went.”'],
    8: ['Imperfect is the background or repeated past habit; preterite is a specific finished event.','Which best means “When I was little, I used to play”?',['Cuando era pequeño, jugaba.','Ayer jugué una vez.'],0,'Jugaba tells a repeated past habit.'],
    9: ['Think of a story as background versus event: imperfect paints the scene, preterite moves the story forward.','Which form paints the background in “It was raining when I arrived”?',['llovía','llegué'],0,'Llovía describes what was underway; llegué is the arrival event.']
   },
   medicine: {
    0: ['A concentration gradient is a hill: particles naturally spread from crowded to less crowded areas. Going uphill needs energy.','What powers a pump moving ions uphill?',['ATP','Gravity'],0,'ATP supplies energy for active transport across a gradient.'],
    1: ['Cells use oxygen at the end of the mitochondrial electron-transport chain. Without enough oxygen, that ATP factory slows.','If oxygen delivery drops severely, mitochondrial ATP production usually…',['falls','rises'],0,'Oxygen is the final electron acceptor; lower oxygen limits oxidative phosphorylation.'],
    2: ['DNA is stored instructions. RNA is a working copy. Ribosomes read RNA to build proteins.','What directly makes proteins from messenger RNA?',['ribosomes','red blood cells'],0,'Ribosomes translate mRNA into protein.'],
    3: ['Negative feedback means a change triggers a response that pushes the variable toward its normal range.','If blood glucose rises, which response helps lower it?',['insulin release','more glucose production'],0,'Insulin helps tissues take up and store glucose, reducing blood glucose.'],

 const sections = new Set(['spanish','piano','medicine','chess','economics']);
 const content = (section,index) => section==='spanish'?V35_SPANISH[index]:section==='piano'?V35_PIANO[index]:section==='medicine'?V35_MEDICINE[index]:section==='chess'?V35_CHESS[index]:section==='economics'?V51_ECON[index]:null;
 function fallbacks(section,d,index){
  if(section==='spanish') return {concept:'Start with the time meaning: '+String(d.why||''),example:'Compare your English task with this authored Spanish model: '+d.en+' → '+d.es,prior:index>0?'Return to the previous tense lesson to rebuild the prerequisite.':'Start with one useful verb and one example.'};
  if(section==='piano') return {concept:'The next sound is '+(d.notes?.[v35.step]||d.notes?.[0]||'the highlighted note')+'. Find it once; there is no speed requirement.',example:'The full exercise is '+d.notes.join(' → ')+'. You can listen to the next note using the button beneath the keyboard.',prior:index>0?'Revisit the previous keyboard lesson and play one note at a time.':'Look for the two-black-key group and the C to its left.'};
  if(section==='medicine') return {concept:d.core,example:'Try applying it to this example: '+(d.case||d.question)+'.',prior:index>0?'The previous medical lesson may explain a needed building block.':'Cells are the starting point for understanding organs.'};
  if(section==='chess') return {concept:d.core,example:'A useful next action: '+(d.action||d.why),prior:index>0?'Review the preceding chess lesson if the rule is unfamiliar.':'First learn to identify each square.'};
  return {concept:d.idea,example:'Here is the situation in the lesson: '+d.example,prior:index>0?'Review the previous economics idea before returning here.':'Start by comparing the real options you must give up.'};
 }
 function placeBridge(root,section,index){
  const d=content(section,index);if(!d)return;
  const task=root.querySelector(section==='economics'?'#v51Question':'.v36-practice');
  if(!task)return;
  const curated=FOUNDATION[section]?.[index];
  const base=fallbacks(section,d,index);
  const details=document.createElement('details');details.className='v55-bridge';details.dataset.v55Bridge=section;
  details.innerHTML='<summary><span class="v55-bridge-icon" aria-hidden="true">↘</span><span><b>Make this simpler</b><small>A smaller step when the lesson feels confusing</small></span></summary><div class="v55-bridge-body"><div class="v55-bridge-choice" role="group" aria-label="Choose what you need help with"><button type="button" data-v55-tab="meaning">What does it mean?</button><button type="button" data-v55-tab="example">Show an example</button><button type="button" data-v55-tab="practice">Give me a smaller question</button></div><div class="v55-bridge-message" role="status" aria-live="polite">Choose the kind of help you want.</div><div class="v55-bridge-actions"><button type="button" data-v55-back>Back to my original task ↑</button></div></div>';
  const existing=task.querySelector('.v54-help'); if(existing)existing.after(details);else task.append(details);
  const message=details.querySelector('.v55-bridge-message');
  const tabs=details.querySelectorAll('[data-v55-tab]');
  function show(tab){v35.help=true;tabs.forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(b.dataset.v55Tab===tab)));
   if(tab==='meaning')message.innerHTML='<strong>One idea at a time</strong><p>'+escape(curated?.[0]||base.concept)+'</p>';
   else if(tab==='example')message.innerHTML='<strong>See it in context</strong><p>'+escape(base.example)+'</p><p class="v55-small">'+escape(base.prior)+'</p>';
   else if(curated){
    const [explanation,q,options,correct,reason]=curated;
    // Rotate answers without relying on one permanently correct location.
    const flip=((index + section.length)%2)===1;const order=flip?[1,0]:[0,1];
    message.innerHTML='<strong>A 20-second check</strong><p>'+escape(explanation)+'</p><p><b>'+escape(q)+'</b></p><div class="v55-mini-options">'+order.map(o=>'<button type="button" data-v55-mini="'+o+'">'+escape(options[o])+'</button>').join('')+'</div><p class="v55-mini-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p><button type="button" class="v55-reveal" data-v55-reveal>Show why</button>';
    const feedback=message.querySelector('.v55-mini-feedback');
    const solution=()=>{feedback.textContent='Why: '+reason;feedback.classList.add('v55-solution')};
    message.querySelectorAll('[data-v55-mini]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
      const answer=Number(b.dataset.v55Mini);if(answer===correct){feedback.textContent='Yes. '+reason+' Now try the original task.';feedback.classList.add('v55-success');message.querySelectorAll('[data-v55-mini]').forEach(x=>x.disabled=true)}
      else {feedback.textContent='Not yet. Think about the explanation above and choose once more. You can reveal the reasoning if you prefer.';feedback.classList.remove('v55-success');b.disabled=true}
    });message.querySelector('[data-v55-reveal]').onclick=solution;
   } else {
    message.innerHTML='<strong>Make the problem smaller</strong><p>'+escape(base.concept)+'</p><p>'+escape(base.example)+'</p><p class="v55-small">'+escape(base.prior)+'</p><button type="button" data-v55-previous '+(index===0?'disabled':'')+'>Review the earlier lesson →</button>';
    const prev=message.querySelector('[data-v55-previous]');if(prev)prev.onclick=()=>v35Open(section,Math.max(0,index-1));
   }
  }
  tabs.forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>show(b.dataset.v55Tab));
  details.querySelector('[data-v55-back]').onclick=()=>{details.open=false;const focus=task.querySelector('#v36Entry')||task.querySelector('[data-v36-choice]:not([disabled])')||task.querySelector('[data-v51-choice]:not([disabled])')||task.querySelector('.v36-keyboard [data-v34-key]');if(focus){focus.focus({preventScroll:true});focus.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'center'})}else task.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'})};
  // A wrong response is a gentle offer, never a modal or forced detour.
  if(section==='medicine'||section==='chess'){
    const list=task.querySelector('.v36-choice-list');if(list){task.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      const btn=e.target.closest('[data-v36-choice]');if(!btn)return;
      if(Number(btn.dataset.v36Choice)!==d.answer){setTimeout(()=>{if(!details.isConnected)return;const retry=task.querySelector('#v53Retry');if(retry&&!retry.hidden){retry.querySelector('[data-v55-offer]')?.remove();const offer=document.createElement('button');offer.type='button';offer.dataset.v55Offer='1';offer.textContent='Try a smaller example →';offer.onclick=()=>{details.open=true;show(curated?'practice':'meaning');details.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'})};retry.append(' ',offer)}},10)}
    },true)}
  } else if(section==='spanish'){
    const btn=task.querySelector('#v36Check');if(btn)btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
       const entered=task.querySelector('#v36Entry')?.value.trim();if(!entered)return;
       const norm=s=>v35N(s).replace(/^yo\s+/,'');if(norm(entered)===norm(d.es))return;
       setTimeout(()=>{
         if(!details.isConnected)return;
         const note=task.querySelector('#v36Message');if(note&&!note.querySelector('[data-v55-offer]')){
           const offer=document.createElement('button');offer.type='button';offer.dataset.v55Offer='1';offer.textContent='Compare a simpler tense example →';offer.onclick=()=>{details.open=true;show(curated?'practice':'meaning');details.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'})};note.append(' ',offer);
         }
       },0);
    });
  } else if(section==='economics'){
    task.querySelectorAll('[data-v51-choice]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      if(Number(btn.dataset.v51Choice)===d.answer)return;
      const feedback=task.querySelector('#v51Feedback');if(!feedback)return;
      setTimeout(()=>{if(!details.isConnected||feedback.querySelector('[data-v55-offer]'))return;const offer=document.createElement('button');offer.type='button';offer.dataset.v55Offer='1';offer.textContent='Build the underlying idea →';offer.onclick=()=>{details.open=true;show(curated?'practice':'meaning');details.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'})};feedback.append(offer)},10);
    }));
  }
 }
 const previousFrame=v35Frame;
 v35Frame=function(markup){previousFrame(markup);const portal=document.getElementById('v35Portal');if(!portal||!sections.has(v35.section))return;
   const root=portal.querySelector('.v36-lesson')||portal.querySelector('.v51-econ-lesson');if(root)placeBridge(root,v35.section,v35.index);
 };
 const oldInit=v35Init;v35Init=function(){oldInit();const label=document.querySelector('header .brand small');if(label)label.textContent='V55 · ONE STEP AT A TIME';};
})();

<script>
/* Study Table V56 — meaningful recall and portable progress, no bogus AI diagnosis. */
(function(){
'use strict';
const STORE='studytable_v56_recall';
const SECTIONS=['spanish','piano','medicine','chess','economics','books'];
const NAMES={spanish:'Spanish',piano:'Piano',medicine:'Medical school',chess:'Chess',economics:'Economics',books:'Books'};
const ARR={spanish:()=>V35_SPANISH,piano:()=>V35_PIANO,medicine:()=>V35_MEDICINE,chess:()=>V35_CHESS,economics:()=>V51_ECON,books:()=>V35_BOOKS};
const E=s=>v35h(String(s??''));
const now=()=>Date.now();const DAY=86400000;
function safeObject(x){return x&&typeof x==='object'&&!Array.isArray(x)?x:{}}
function read(){try{return safeObject(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE)||'{}'))}catch{return {}}}
function write(data){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(data));return true}catch{return false}}
function byKey(key){const [subject,si]=String(key).split(':');const index=Number(si);return SECTIONS.includes(subject)&&Number.isInteger(index)&&index>=0&&index<ARR[subject]().length?{section:subject,index,item:ARR[subject]()[index]}:null}
function register(key,type,meta={}){
 if(!byKey(key))return;
 const data=read(),p=safeObject(data[key]);p.attempts=(p.attempts||0)+1;p.last=now();
 if(type==='struggle') {p.struggles=(p.struggles||0)+1;p.streak=0;p.due=now();p.flag=true;p.lastResult='needs-work';}
 else if(type==='assisted'){p.assisted=(p.assisted||0)+1;p.streak=0;p.due=now();p.flag=true;p.lastResult='assisted';}
 else if(type==='independent'){p.independent=(p.independent||0)+1;p.streak=Math.min(5,(p.streak||0)+1);p.due=now()+([0,2,4,8,15,30][p.streak])*DAY;p.flag=false;p.lastResult='independent';}
 else if(type==='manual'){p.flag=true;p.due=now();p.lastResult='manual';}
 if(meta.source)p.source=String(meta.source).slice(0,40);
 data[key]=p;write(data);return p;
}
function newKey(){return ['spanish','medicine','chess','economics','piano'].map(s=>s+':0');}
function candidates(){
 const p=v35Progress(),rec=read(),keys=new Set([...Object.keys(rec),...Object.keys(p)].filter(byKey));
 const nowDate=now();return [...keys].map(key=>{
  const prog=p[key],data=safeObject(rec[key]),ever=!!(prog?.seen||data.attempts), mastered=!!prog?.mastered;
  if(!ever&&!data.flag)return null;
  let due=Number.isFinite(data.due)?data.due:(mastered?Date.parse(prog.date||'')+7*DAY:0);if(!Number.isFinite(due))due=0;
  const isDue=due<=nowDate||!Number.isFinite(due);
  if(!isDue&&!data.flag)return null;
  const info=byKey(key);if(!info)return null;
  const priority=(data.flag?200:0)+(!mastered?70:0)+(data.struggles||0)*8+(data.assisted||0)*4+(isDue?20:0)+Math.min(10,Math.max(0,(nowDate-due)/DAY));
  return {...info,key,priority,due:due||0,attempts:data.attempts||0,flag:!!data.flag};
 }).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority||a.due-b.due);
}
function title(section,d){return d.title||d.en||'Lesson';}
function describeTime(ts){if(!ts||ts<=now())return 'ready now';const days=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((ts-now())/DAY));return 'in '+days+' day'+(days===1?'':'s')}
let active=null;
function reviewHome(){const due=candidates(),weak=due.filter(x=>x.flag).length;
 const progress=v35Progress(),done=Object.keys(progress).filter(k=>byKey(k)&&progress[k]?.mastered).length;
 return '<section class="v56-review-home"><div class="v56-review-symbol" aria-hidden="true">↻</div><div class="v56-review-copy"><strong>Remember what you learned</strong><p>'+ (due.length?`${due.length} idea${due.length===1?'':'s'} ready for a quick review${weak?' · '+weak+' need another look':''}.`:'No ideas are due. Study one lesson and your personal review list will begin to grow.') +'</p><small>'+done+' lessons completed independently · No streaks, no deadlines.</small></div><button type="button" class="v56-main-btn" id="v56ReviewEntry">'+(due.length?'Review '+Math.min(5,due.length)+' '+(Math.min(5,due.length)===1?'idea':'ideas')+' →':'Open my review room →')+'</button></section>';
}
function updateNav(){const nav=document.querySelector('#v35Portal .v35-nav')||document.querySelector('#v35Portal .v36-head-actions');if(nav&&!nav.querySelector('#v56Nav')){const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.id='v56Nav';btn.textContent='My review';btn.type='button';btn.setAttribute('aria-current',v35.section==='review'?'page':'false');btn.onclick=()=>openReview();nav.prepend(btn)}}
function addLaterButton(){if(!SECTIONS.includes(v35.section)||v35.section==='books')return;
 const root=document.querySelector('#v35Portal .v36-lesson, #v35Portal .v51-econ-lesson');if(!root||root.querySelector('[data-v56-later]'))return;
 const task=root.querySelector('.v36-practice,#v51Question');if(!task)return;
 const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='v56-save-row';row.innerHTML='<button type="button" data-v56-later>Remember to practice this again</button><span data-v56-later-note aria-live="polite"></span>';
 task.append(row);row.querySelector('button').onclick=()=>{register(v35.section+':'+v35.index,'manual',{source:'learner flag'});row.querySelector('span').textContent='Added to your review list.';row.querySelector('button').disabled=true};
}
// Capture events before the legacy answer handler; never judge Spanish synonyms as errors.
const sessionState={key:null,wrong:false,help:false,reported:false};
function resetSession(){const key=v35.section+':'+v35.index;if(sessionState.key===key)return;sessionState.key=key;sessionState.wrong=false;sessionState.help=false;sessionState.reported=false}
function capture(e){const portal=document.getElementById('v35Portal');if(!portal||portal.hidden||!SECTIONS.includes(v35.section))return;
 const target=e.target.closest('button,summary,input');if(!target||!portal.contains(target))return;
 resetSession();const section=v35.section,key=section+':'+v35.index;
 const choice=target.closest('[data-v36-choice]');if(choice&&(section==='medicine'||section==='chess')){
   const d=ARR[section]()[v35.index];if(Number(choice.dataset.v36Choice)!==d.answer&&!sessionState.wrong){sessionState.wrong=true;register(key,'struggle',{source:'wrong choice'})}
 }
 const econ=target.closest('[data-v51-choice]');if(econ&&section==='economics'){
   const d=V51_ECON[v35.index];if(Number(econ.dataset.v51Choice)!==d.answer&&!sessionState.wrong){sessionState.wrong=true;register(key,'struggle',{source:'wrong choice'})}
 }
 const piano=target.closest('[data-v34-key]');if(piano&&section==='piano'){
   const d=V35_PIANO[v35.index];if(piano.dataset.v34Key!==d.notes[v35.step]&&!sessionState.wrong){sessionState.wrong=true;register(key,'struggle',{source:'wrong piano key'})}
 }
 const helper=target.closest('[data-v54-stage],[data-v55-tab],#v36Reveal,#v36Hint,#v36ShowModule,#v36HearNote,#v51Hint,#v51Reveal,#v36ShowPiano,#v36ExplainModule');
 if(helper)sessionState.help=true;
}
document.addEventListener('click',capture,true);
// Compatible with existing V35/V54 assisted-vs-independent progress semantics.
const originalSave=v35Save;
v35Save=function(key,independent=false){const out=originalSave(key,independent);if(!byKey(key))return out;
 resetSession();if(sessionState.reported)return out;
 // Hints/false-negative Spanish checking aren't equivalent to knowledge. Mark as assisted.
 const independently=!!independent&&!sessionState.help&&!sessionState.wrong;
 register(key,independently?'independent':'assisted',{source:'guided lesson'});
 sessionState.reported=true;return out;};
// Old handler may mark the model mismatch as assisted, but never as definitively wrong.
function escapeReview(s){return E(s)}
function cardHtml(t){const d=t.item,sec=t.section;
 if(sec==='spanish')return `<label class="v56-prompt" for="v56Spanish">Say this in Spanish, then type it if you want.</label><p class="v56-question">${E(d.en)}</p><textarea id="v56Spanish" rows="2" placeholder="Your Spanish sentence (optional)"></textarea><div class="v56-note">Tense: ${E(d.phase)}. This offline app checks against an authored example, not every valid translation.</div>`;
 if(sec==='piano')return `<p class="v56-question">Play the first ${Math.min(3,d.notes.length)} notes of “${E(d.title)}” from memory.</p><p>You may press the keys below. No timer. If you want to hear the target note or see the sequence, reveal the explanation.</p><div class="v56-piano-keys">${v35RealKeyboard()}</div><p id="v56PianoMsg" role="status">Played: none yet</p>`;
 if(sec==='books')return `<p class="v56-question">What is one important idea from <em>${E(d.title)}</em> by ${E(d.author)}?</p><textarea id="v56RecallText" rows="3" placeholder="Try recalling it in your own words first (optional)"></textarea>`;
 const opts=d.options.map((answer,idx)=>({answer,idx}));const offset=((t.index+(active?.pos||0)+(active?.seed||0))%opts.length);const sorted=opts.slice(offset).concat(opts.slice(0,offset));
 return `<p class="v56-question">${E(d.question)}</p><div class="v56-choices">${sorted.map((o,i)=>`<button type="button" data-v56-choice="${o.idx}"><span>${String.fromCharCode(65+i)}</span>${E(o.answer)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}
function answerHtml(t){const d=t.item,sec=t.section;
 if(sec==='spanish')return `<strong>One natural model answer</strong><p lang="es">${E(d.es)}</p><p>${E(d.explanation)}</p><p class="v56-soft">Your answer can differ and still be correct. Rate whether you could express the meaning, not whether every word matched.</p>`;
 if(sec==='piano')return `<strong>Model notes</strong><p>${d.notes.slice(0,3).map(E).join(' → ')}</p><p>${E(d.why)}</p>`;
 if(sec==='books')return `<strong>Core ideas from the reading guide</strong><p>${E(d.ideas)}</p><details><summary>More context</summary><p>${E(d.brief)}</p></details>`;
 return `<strong>Best answer: ${E(d.options[d.answer])}</strong><p>${E(d.why)}</p>${sec==='medicine'?'<small>For education only, not clinical advice.</small>':''}`;
}
function showAnswer(){if(!active)return;active.revealed=true;const t=active.queue[active.pos];const answer=document.querySelector('#v56Answer');if(!answer)return;answer.hidden=false;answer.innerHTML=answerHtml(t);const rate=document.getElementById('v56Rate');rate.hidden=false;document.getElementById('v56Reveal').hidden=true;const yes=rate.querySelector('[data-v56-rate="knew"]');if(yes&&(['medicine','chess','economics'].includes(t.section)&&active.pressed===null||t.section==='piano'&&!active.pianoCorrect)){yes.disabled=true;yes.textContent='Practice this again';yes.title='Try the task before revealing the answer'}answer.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});}
function nextReview(confident){const t=active?.queue[active.pos];if(!t)return;
 register(t.key,confident?'independent':'struggle',{source:'recall review'});
 // Correct feedback is a self-rating for open responses and piano, not an automatic score.
 active.pos++;active.revealed=false;active.pressed=null;active.pianoCorrect=false;renderReview();
}
function renderReview(){const host=document.getElementById('v35Portal');if(!host)return;
 if(!active||active.pos>=active.queue.length){
  const finished=!!active&&active.queue.length>0;
  v35Frame(`<section class="v56-review-screen"><span class="v36-kicker">YOUR LEARNING REVIEW</span><h1>${finished?'Nice work. You reviewed '+active.queue.length+' ideas.':'Your memory room'}</h1><p>${finished?'You have a new next-review date for each idea. Ones needing practice can return sooner.':'No questions are due yet. Learning something new is the next useful step.'}</p><div class="v56-actions"><button type="button" class="v56-main-btn" id="v56ToHome">Choose a subject →</button><button type="button" id="v56SeeList">See my progress</button></div><div id="v56BackupDock"></div></section>`);
  document.getElementById('v56ToHome').onclick=()=>v35Open('home');document.getElementById('v56SeeList').onclick=renderReviewList;makeBackupButtons();return;
 }
 const t=active.queue[active.pos];v35Frame(`<section class="v56-review-screen"><div class="v56-review-top"><span>${E(NAMES[t.section])} · ${active.pos+1} of ${active.queue.length}</span><button id="v56Quit">Exit review</button></div><div class="v56-review-meter"><i style="width:${Math.round(100*active.pos/active.queue.length)}%"></i></div><h1>Try remembering before you look.</h1><p class="v56-intro">You can think silently or answer aloud. If you need the explanation, reveal it—there is no penalty.</p><div class="v56-review-card">${cardHtml(t)}<div class="v56-actions"><button id="v56Reveal" class="v56-main-btn">Show the answer and why</button></div><div id="v56Answer" class="v56-review-answer" hidden></div><div id="v56Rate" class="v56-rate" hidden><p><b>How did that feel?</b></p><div class="v56-actions"><button data-v56-rate="retry">I need another look</button><button data-v56-rate="knew" class="v56-main-btn">I knew it without help</button></div><small>Be generous with yourself, but count “I knew it” only if you understood it before revealing the answer.</small></div></div></section>`);
 document.getElementById('v56Quit').onclick=()=>v35Open('home');document.getElementById('v56Reveal').onclick=showAnswer;
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v56-rate]').forEach(x=>x.onclick=()=>nextReview(x.dataset.v56Rate==='knew'&&!active.pressed));
 if(t.section==='piano'){
  const played=[];const n=Math.min(3,t.item.notes.length);
  document.querySelectorAll('.v56-piano-keys [data-v34-key]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{
    v27Sound(btn.dataset.v34Key);played.push(btn.dataset.v34Key);if(played.length>n)played.shift();
    const correct=played.length===n&&played.every((key,j)=>key===t.item.notes[j]);
    document.getElementById('v56PianoMsg').textContent=correct?'You played the short sequence correctly. Check the explanation when ready.':'Played '+played.length+' of '+n+' notes. Take your time.';
    if(correct){active.pianoCorrect=true;btn.classList.add('v56-played')}
  });
 }
 if(['medicine','chess','economics'].includes(t.section)){
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-v56-choice]').forEach(x=>x.onclick=()=>{
   if(active.pressed!==null)return;active.pressed=Number(x.dataset.v56Choice)!==t.item.answer;
   x.classList.add(active.pressed?'v56-wrong':'v56-right');
   document.querySelectorAll('[data-v56-choice]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);
   showAnswer();
   // The answer was actually checked; correct first attempt can be rated independently.
   const yes=document.querySelector('[data-v56-rate="knew"]');if(yes&&active.pressed){yes.disabled=true;yes.title='This first attempt was incorrect';yes.textContent='Try again in review';}
  });
 }
}
function renderReviewList(){active=null;const data=read(),progress=v35Progress();const keys=Object.keys(data).filter(byKey).sort((a,b)=>(data[a].due||0)-(data[b].due||0));
 const items=keys.map(key=>{const t=byKey(key),r=data[key],p=progress[key]||{};return `<li><div><strong>${E(NAMES[t.section])} · ${E(title(t.section,t.item))}</strong><small>${r.flag?'Needs another look':p.mastered?'Did it without help':'Practiced with help'} · Next review ${E(describeTime(r.due))}</small></div><button data-v56-open="${E(key)}">Open lesson →</button></li>`}).join('')||'<li>No review history yet. When you complete lessons or request help, this list will grow.</li>';
 v35Frame(`<section class="v56-review-screen"><span class="v36-kicker">PERSONAL REVIEW</span><h1>What should I revisit?</h1><p>These dates reflect what you reported or demonstrated in Study Table. They are suggestions, not grades.</p><ul class="v56-review-list">${items}</ul><div class="v56-actions"><button class="v56-main-btn" id="v56BackReview">Start my review →</button><button id="v56BackHome">All subjects</button></div><div id="v56BackupDock"></div></section>`);
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v56-open]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const t=byKey(b.dataset.v56Open);if(t)v35Open(t.section,t.index)});
 document.getElementById('v56BackReview').onclick=startReview;document.getElementById('v56BackHome').onclick=()=>v35Open('home');makeBackupButtons();
}
function startReview(){const q=candidates().slice(0,5);active={queue:q,pos:0,seed:Math.floor(now()/DAY),pressed:null,revealed:false};v35.section='review';renderReview();}
function openReview(){v35Open('home');v35.section='review';active=null;renderReviewList();}
const BACKUP_KEYS=['studytable_v35_progress','studytable_v54_teachback','studytable_v51_notes','studytable_v57_practice','studytable_v62_discoveries','studytable_v63_loops','studytable_v64_think_aloud','studytable_v65_sessions','studytable_v65_verbs','studytable_v66_conversations','studytable_v67_piano','studytable_v70_lab','studytable_v72_chess','studytable_v73_coach','studytable_v74_diagnostics','studytable_v76_coach','studytable_v77_compass','studytable_v78_coach','studytable_v81_written','studytable_v83_cases','studytable_v84_case_notebook','studytable_v85_case_review','studytable_v86_practice','studytable_v89_guided','studytable_v90_preferences','studytable_v91_today','studytable_v91_chess','studytable_v91_spanish','studytable_v91_piano','studytable_v92_verbs','studytable:v100:recall','studytable_v98_recall','studytable:v102:mastery','studytable_v102_learning',STORE];
function exportProgress(){const records={};for(const k of BACKUP_KEYS){try{const v=localStorage.getItem(k);if(v!==null)records[k]=JSON.parse(v)}catch{}}
 const data=JSON.stringify({format:'studytable-progress',version:57,exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),records},null,2);
 const blob=new Blob([data],{type:'application/json'});const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='study-table-my-progress.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),500);}
function makeBackupButtons(){const dock=document.getElementById('v56BackupDock');if(!dock)return;
 dock.innerHTML='<details class="v56-backup"><summary>Back up or restore my progress</summary><p>Progress is saved in this browser. Export a small JSON backup when changing devices or moving between HTML editions.</p><button type="button" id="v56Export">Save my progress to a file</button><label class="v56-import">Restore a progress file<input type="file" id="v56Import" accept="application/json,.json"></label><p class="v56-backup-message" role="status"></p></details>';
 dock.querySelector('#v56Export').onclick=exportProgress;
 dock.querySelector('#v56Import').onchange=async e=>{const file=e.target.files?.[0];if(!file)return;const msg=dock.querySelector('.v56-backup-message');
 try{const result=await window.studyTableV94SafeRestore.restore(file);msg.textContent=result.message;
 const undo=document.createElement('button');undo.type='button';undo.textContent='Undo this restore';undo.onclick=()=>{try{msg.textContent=window.studyTableV94SafeRestore.undo()}catch(err){msg.textContent=String(err.message||err)}};msg.append(' ',undo)
 }catch(err){msg.textContent='Could not restore: '+String(err.message||err)}
 };
}
const oldOpen=v35Open;
v35Open=function(...args){sessionState.key=null;sessionState.wrong=false;sessionState.help=false;sessionState.reported=false;return oldOpen(...args)};
const oldFrame=v35Frame;
v35Frame=function(markup){oldFrame(markup);updateNav();addLaterButton();};
const oldHome=v35Home;
v35Home=function(){oldHome();const hero=document.querySelector('#v35Portal .v36-home-hero');if(!hero)return;
 const el=document.createElement('div');el.innerHTML=reviewHome();const block=el.firstElementChild;
 const h=hero.querySelector('h1');if(candidates().length&&h)h.after(block);else hero.append(block);
 block.querySelector('#v56ReviewEntry').onclick=()=>{if(candidates().length){v35.section='review';startReview()}else openReview()};
};
const oldInit=v35Init;v35Init=function(){oldInit();const label=document.querySelector('header .brand small');if(label)label.textContent='V56 · REMEMBER WHAT YOU LEARN';};
window.studyTableV56={openReview,startReview,register,read,candidates,openList:renderReviewList};
})();
