 const MODES={listen:{name:'Listen & understand',length:LISTEN.length},ear:{name:'Hear the difference',length:EAR.length},apply:{name:'Apply what you know',length:TRANSFER.length}};
 const MODES_ORDER=['listen','ear','apply'];
 const OFFSETS={listen:0,ear:0,apply:0};
 let ui={mode:'listen',index:0,wrong:false,help:false,answered:false,heard:false,playedA:false,playedB:false};
 function load(){try{const x=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE)||'{}');return x&&typeof x==='object'&&!Array.isArray(x)?x:{}}catch{return {}}}
 function save(data){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(data))}catch{}}
 function status(mode,i){return load()[mode+':'+i]||null}
 function record(mode,i,type){const d=load(),k=mode+':'+i,p=d[k]||{attempts:0,independent:0,assisted:0};p.attempts++;p[type]=(p[type]||0)+1;p.last=Date.now();d[k]=p;save(d)}
 function nextUnfinished(mode){const n=MODES[mode].length;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)if(!status(mode,i)?.independent)return i;return 0}
 function lessonLink(item){return `<button type="button" data-v57-foundation="${item.subject}:${item.foundation}">Review the related ${E(item.subject)} lesson →</button>`}
 function nav(){return `<div class="v57-nav"><button type="button" id="v57Home">← Learning home</button><span>Optional practice room · no timers or streaks</span></div>`}
 function top(){return `${nav()}<div class="v57-progress"><small>${ui.index+1} of ${MODES[ui.mode].length} · ${E(MODES[ui.mode].name)}</small><div aria-hidden="true"><i style="width:${((ui.index+1)/MODES[ui.mode].length*100).toFixed(2)}%"></i></div></div>`}
 function audio(lang,text,rate){if(!('speechSynthesis' in window)||typeof SpeechSynthesisUtterance==='undefined')return false;try{const u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);u.lang=lang;u.rate=rate;window.speechSynthesis.cancel();window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);return true}catch{return false}}
 function render(){
  const mode=ui.mode,i=ui.index,x=mode==='listen'?LISTEN[i]:mode==='ear'?EAR[i]:TRANSFER[i];
  v35.section='practice';
  let body='';
  if(mode==='listen')body=`<div class="v57-intro"><span class="v36-kicker">SPANISH LISTENING · ${E(x.focus)}</span><h1>Listen for the meaning.</h1><p>Press Play. Listen once or twice. Choose what you heard. You can show the words whenever you need them.</p></div><div class="v57-hear"><button type="button" id="v57Listen" class="v57-main">▶ Play Spanish</button><button type="button" id="v57Slow">Play slowly</button><button type="button" id="v57Transcript">Show written Spanish</button><p id="v57AudioNote" role="status">Audio uses your browser’s Spanish voice, if available.</p><p id="v57Text" lang="es" hidden>${E(x.es)}</p></div><div class="v57-question">Which English meaning matches the Spanish?</div>${options(x.options,x.correct)}<div class="v57-why" id="v57Why" hidden><strong>${E(x.options[x.correct])}</strong><p lang="es">${E(x.es)}</p><p>${E(x.why)}</p><div class="v57-reply"><h3>Say something back (optional)</h3><p>${E(x.reply)}</p><textarea id="v57SpeakText" rows="2" placeholder="Say your answer, then type it if you want"></textarea><button type="button" id="v57Model">See one natural reply</button><p id="v57ModelText" hidden lang="es">${E(x.model)}</p><small>Alternative Spanish sentences can also be correct. This is practice, not automated pronunciation grading.</small></div></div>`;
  if(mode==='ear')body=`<div class="v57-intro"><span class="v36-kicker">PIANO · LEARN WITH YOUR EARS</span><h1>Is the second note higher or lower?</h1><p>First play A, then B. No keyboard knowledge is needed: you're training your ear to recognize how a sound moves.</p></div><div class="v57-hear"><div class="v57-audio-pair"><button class="v57-main" id="v57A">▶ Note A</button><button class="v57-main" id="v57B">▶ Note B</button></div><button id="v57HearHelp">Show which keys are playing</button><p id="v57Notes" hidden>${E(x.a)} → ${E(x.b)}. ${E(x.tip)}</p></div><div class="v57-question">Compared with A, is B…</div>${options(['Higher','Lower','The same'],['higher','lower','same'].indexOf(x.correct))}<div class="v57-why" id="v57Why" hidden><strong>${x.correct==='same'?'The same pitch':x.correct==='higher'?'Higher pitch':'Lower pitch'}</strong><p>${E(x.why)}</p><p class="v57-small">Try humming A, then B. You can replay both tones.</p></div>`;
  if(mode==='apply')body=`<div class="v57-intro"><span class="v36-kicker">${E(x.subject.toUpperCase())} · ${E(x.track)}</span><h1>Think through a new situation.</h1><p>This isn't the same question as your lesson. Apply the underlying principle before checking the explanation.</p></div><div class="v57-scene">${E(x.scene)}</div><div class="v57-question">${E(x.question)}</div>${options(x.options,x.answer)}<div class="v57-why" id="v57Why" hidden><strong>Best answer: ${E(x.options[x.answer])}</strong><p>${E(x.why)}</p><p class="v57-pitfall"><b>Easy mistake:</b> ${E(x.pitfall)}</p>${lessonLink(x)}${x.subject==='medicine'?'<small>Concept exercise only; not medical advice or a diagnostic tool.</small>':''}</div>`;
  const counts=Object.keys(load()).filter(k=>k.startsWith(mode+':')&&load()[k].independent).length;
  v35Frame(`<section class="v57-room">${top()}${body}<div class="v57-result" id="v57Feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div><div class="v57-actions"><button type="button" id="v57Answer" class="v57-link">Show me the reasoning</button><button type="button" id="v57Next" class="v57-main" hidden>Next practice →</button></div><div class="v57-bottom"><button type="button" id="v57Previous" ${i===0?'disabled':''}>← Previous</button><span>${counts} of ${MODES[mode].length} solved without help</span><button type="button" id="v57Exit">Other practice rooms</button></div></section>`);
  wire(x);
 }
 function options(items,answer){const rotate=(ui.index*2+ui.mode.length)%items.length;const order=items.map((_,i)=>i);const sequence=order.slice(rotate).concat(order.slice(0,rotate));return `<div class="v57-options">${sequence.map((original,j)=>`<button type="button" data-v57-option="${original}"><span>${String.fromCharCode(65+j)}</span>${E(items[original])}</button>`).join('')}</div>`}
 function success(correct,x){const panel=document.getElementById('v57Feedback');if(!panel)return;
  const mode=ui.mode,soundTried=mode==='listen'?ui.heard:mode==='ear'?(ui.playedA&&ui.playedB):true,independent=correct&&!ui.wrong&&!ui.help&&soundTried;
  if(correct){panel.textContent=independent?'Correct on your own. That goes into your practice history.':!soundTried?'Correct. Next time play both sounds first to test listening.':'You got there. This was guided practice, and that counts too.';panel.className='v57-result good';}
  else {panel.innerHTML='Not yet. Try another choice. You can replay or ask to see the reasoning.';panel.className='v57-result gentle';return;}
  record(mode,ui.index,independent?'independent':'assisted');finish(x);
 }
 function finish(x){ui.answered=true;document.getElementById('v57Why').hidden=false;document.getElementById('v57Next').hidden=false;document.getElementById('v57Answer').hidden=true;document.querySelectorAll('[data-v57-option]').forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;const idx=Number(b.dataset.v57Option),correct=ui.mode==='listen'?x.correct:ui.mode==='ear'?['higher','lower','same'].indexOf(x.correct):x.answer;if(idx===correct)b.classList.add('right')});wirePost(x)}
 function wirePost(x){document.querySelector('[data-v57-foundation]')?.addEventListener('click',e=>{const [s,j]=e.currentTarget.dataset.v57Foundation.split(':');v35Open(s,Number(j))});
 document.getElementById('v57Model')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{document.getElementById('v57ModelText').hidden=false});}
 function wire(x){document.getElementById('v57Home').onclick=()=>v35Open('home');document.getElementById('v57Exit').onclick=()=>showMenu();document.getElementById('v57Previous').onclick=()=>open(ui.mode,ui.index-1);document.getElementById('v57Next').onclick=()=>open(ui.mode,(ui.index+1)%MODES[ui.mode].length);
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-v57-option]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(ui.answered)return;const ans=Number(b.dataset.v57Option),right=ui.mode==='listen'?x.correct:ui.mode==='ear'?['higher','lower','same'].indexOf(x.correct):x.answer;if(ans!==right){ui.wrong=true;b.disabled=true;b.classList.add('wrong');success(false,x)}else{b.classList.add('right');success(true,x)}});
  document.getElementById('v57Answer').onclick=()=>{if(ui.answered)return;ui.help=true;record(ui.mode,ui.index,'assisted');document.getElementById('v57Feedback').textContent='The solution is below. Try it again without hints another day.';finish(x)};
  if(ui.mode==='listen'){
   const status=document.getElementById('v57AudioNote');const speak=(rate)=>{ui.heard=true;if(!audio('es-MX',x.es,rate))status.textContent='Speech is unavailable in this browser. Use “Show written Spanish” and keep learning.';else status.textContent='Listen for the verb and the time reference.'};
   document.getElementById('v57Listen').onclick=()=>speak(.9);document.getElementById('v57Slow').onclick=()=>speak(.65);document.getElementById('v57Transcript').onclick=()=>{ui.help=true;document.getElementById('v57Text').hidden=false;document.getElementById('v57Transcript').disabled=true};
  } else if(ui.mode==='ear'){
   document.getElementById('v57A').onclick=()=>{ui.playedA=true;v27Sound(x.a)};document.getElementById('v57B').onclick=()=>{ui.playedB=true;v27Sound(x.b)};
   document.getElementById('v57HearHelp').onclick=()=>{ui.help=true;document.getElementById('v57Notes').hidden=false;document.getElementById('v57HearHelp').disabled=true};
  }
 }
 function open(mode,index){if(!MODES[mode])return;const n=MODES[mode].length;ui={mode,index:Math.min(Math.max(0,Number.isInteger(index)?index:nextUnfinished(mode)),n-1),wrong:false,help:false,answered:false,heard:false,playedA:false,playedB:false};render()}
 function showMenu(){v35.section='practice';const cards=MODES_ORDER.map(mode=>{const m=MODES[mode],count=Array.from({length:m.length},(_,i)=>status(mode,i)?.independent?1:0).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);const text={listen:'Hear a sentence, recognize the tense and meaning, then form a response.',ear:'Hear two real notes and decide whether the pitch moves up, down, or stays.',apply:'Work through new medical and economics situations with explained answers.'}[mode];return `<button type="button" class="v57-card" data-v57-go="${mode}"><strong>${E(m.name)}</strong><span>${text}</span><small>${count} of ${m.length} solved independently · Continue →</small></button>`}).join('');v35Frame(`<section class="v57-room v57-menu">${nav()}<span class="v36-kicker">PRACTICE IN THE REAL WORLD</span><h1>Can you use it without the answer in front of you?</h1><p>Pick one small challenge. Listen, think, try, and see why. These are optional: your regular courses stay exactly where they are.</p><div class="v57-cards">${cards}</div><p class="v57-small">The listening lab needs your browser’s text-to-speech voice; piano tones play through its audio engine. If audio is unavailable, the written clues remain accessible. These are authored exercises, not automated assessment of fluency or clinical competence.</p></section>`);document.querySelectorAll('[data-v57-go]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>open(b.dataset.v57Go));document.getElementById('v57Home').onclick=()=>v35Open('home')}
 // Add one optional entry near the current subject's other enrichment, never above the first learning task.
 const oldFrame=v35Frame;
 v35Frame=function(html){oldFrame(html);if(!['spanish','piano','medicine','economics'].includes(v35.section))return;const root=document.querySelector('#v35Portal .v36-lesson,#v35Portal .v51-econ-lesson');if(!root||root.querySelector('[data-v57-launch]'))return;
  const mode=v35.section==='spanish'?'listen':v35.section==='piano'?'ear':'apply';const dock=document.createElement('div');dock.className='v57-dock';dock.innerHTML=`<button type="button" data-v57-launch>Try a fresh ${mode==='listen'?'listening':mode==='ear'?'ear-training':'application'} challenge →</button><small>Optional · No change to this lesson</small>`;root.append(dock);dock.querySelector('button').onclick=()=>open(mode);
 };
 const oldHome=v35Home;v35Home=function(){oldHome();const path=document.querySelector('#v35Portal .v36-library');if(!path)return;const dock=document.createElement('div');dock.className='v57-entry';dock.innerHTML='<b>Ready to try something new?</b><p>Practice what you learned through listening, music, or fresh problems.</p><button type="button" id="v57Start">Try a real-world skill →</button>';path.before(dock);dock.querySelector('button').onclick=showMenu};
 const oldInit=v35Init;v35Init=function(){oldInit();const header=document.querySelector('header .brand small');if(header)header.textContent='V57 · LEARN IT, THEN USE IT';};
 window.studyTableV57={open,showMenu,load,LISTEN,EAR,TRANSFER};
})();

function audio(){try{const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return false;if(!audioCtx)audioCtx=new AC();if(audioCtx.state==='suspended')audioCtx.resume();return true}catch{return false}}
function clickSound(accent){if(!audio()||!audioCtx)return;try{const o=audioCtx.createOscillator(),g=audioCtx.createGain();o.type='sine';o.frequency.value=accent?880:620;g.gain.setValueAtTime(.001,audioCtx.currentTime);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.20,audioCtx.currentTime+.006);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,audioCtx.currentTime+.11);o.connect(g);g.connect(audioCtx.destination);o.start();o.stop(audioCtx.currentTime+.12)}catch{}}
function rhythm(i){cleanup();const dat=memory();i=Number.isInteger(i)?i:RHYTHMS.findIndex((_,j)=>!dat['rhythm:'+j]?.independent);if(i<0)i=0;state={kind:'rhythm',i,revealed:false,answered:false};const x=RHYTHMS[i],hits=new Set(x.hits);const parts=Array.from({length:x.beats},(_,j)=>`<div class="v58-beat ${hits.has(j)?'strong':'rest'}" id="v58Beat${j}" aria-label="Beat ${j+1}: ${hits.has(j)?'tap':'rest'}">${hits.has(j)?'●':'○'}<small style="display:block;font-size:11px">${j%4+1}</small></div>`).join('');frame(`${nav('PIANO RHYTHM',i,RHYTHMS.length)}<div class="v58-eyebrow">STEP ${i+1} · ${x.bpm} BPM</div><h1>${E(x.title)}</h1><div class="v58-panel"><p>${E(x.help)}</p><div class="v58-beats" id="v58Beats">${parts}</div><p class="v58-quiet">● = tap · ○ = rest. The numbers are the beat counts.  const interval=60000/x.bpm,offset=()=>performance.now();const timer=(fn,t)=>timers.push(setTimeout(fn,t));
 function flash(k){beatEls.forEach((b,j)=>b.classList.toggle('active',j===k));}
 document.getElementById('v58Demo').onclick=()=>{cleanup();audio();demoHeard=true;feedback.textContent='Listen: high clicks show beats to play; count the silent beats too.';x.hits.forEach(h=>timer(()=>clickSound(h===0),350+h*interval));for(let b=0;b<x.beats;b++)timer(()=>flash(b),350+b*interval);timer(()=>flash(-1),350+x.beats*interval)};
 document.getElementById('v58Guide').onclick=()=>{visible=!visible;if(run&&visible)guideUsed=true;beatEls.forEach((b,j)=>{b.textContent=visible?(hits.has(j)?'●':'○'):String(j%4+1);b.classList.toggle('strong',visible&&hits.has(j));b.classList.toggle('rest',visible&&!hits.has(j))});document.getElementById('v58Guide').textContent=visible?'Hide the target beats':'Show the target beats';feedback.textContent=visible?'Guided mode: the target beats are visible.':'Independent mode: count the beats without visible target marks.'};
 document.getElementById('v58Start').onclick=()=>{cleanup();audio();taps=[];miss=0;pressCount=0;guideUsed=visible;run=true;state.answered=false;const tap=document.getElementById('v58Tap');tap.disabled=false;tap.textContent='TAP ON THE BEAT';const prep=350;beatStart=offset()+prep+4*interval;feedback.textContent='Count in: 1, 2, 3, 4… then tap the pattern.';for(let k=0;k<4;k++)timer(()=>{counter.textContent='Count-in · '+(k+1)+' of 4';clickSound(k===0)},prep+k*interval);for(let k=0;k<x.beats;k++)timer(()=>{counter.textContent='Play · beat '+(k+1)+' of '+x.beats;flash(k);if(k===0)clickSound(true)},prep+(k+4)*interval);timer(()=>{run=false;tap.disabled=true;flash(-1);const tol=Math.max(185,interval*.34),errors=[];for(let k=0;k<x.hits.length;k++){if(k>=taps.length){errors.push('missing');continue}errors.push(Math.abs(taps[k]-(beatStart+x.hits[k]*interval))<=tol?'ok':'late')}const passed=taps.length===x.hits.length&&errors.every(e=>e==='ok');const assisted=guideUsed||!demoHeard||!passed;save('rhythm:'+i,passed&&!assisted?'independent':'assisted');state.answered=true;feedback.textContent=passed?(assisted?'Nice steady timing! const STORAGE='studytable_v57_practice';

function mark(id,kind){const data=memory(),d=data[id]||{attempts:0,independent:0,assisted:0};d.attempts++;d[kind]=(d[kind]||0)+1;d.last=Date.now();data[id]=d;try{localStorage.setItem(STORAGE,JSON.stringify(data))}catch{}}
function next(arr,prefix){const m=memory();const i=arr.findIndex((_,j)=>!m[prefix+':'+j]?.independent);return i<0?0:i}

const freq={C4:261.63,'C#4':277.18,D4:293.66,'D#4':311.13,E4:329.63,F4:349.23,'F#4':369.99,G4:392,'G#4':415.3,A4:440,'A#4':466.16,B4:493.88,C5:523.25};
function audio(){try{const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return null;if(!ctx)ctx=new AC();if(ctx.state==='suspended')ctx.resume();return ctx}catch{return null}}
function tone(note,time,duration=.31){const c=audio();if(!c||!freq[note])return false;const start=(typeof time==='number'?time:c.currentTime+.005),o=c.createOscillator(),gain=c.createGain();o.type='triangle';o.frequency.value=freq[note];gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.001,start);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.14,start+.016);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,start+duration);o.connect(gain);gain.connect(c.destination);o.start(start);o.stop(start+duration+.02);return true}
function piano(i){i=Number.isInteger(i)?Math.max(0,Math.min(SONGS.length-1,i)):next(SONGS,'melody');const x=SONGS[i],keys=['C4','D4','E4','F4','G4','A4','B4','C5'],black=[['C#4',12.5],['D#4',25],['F#4',50],['G#4',62.5],['A#4',75]];let notesDone=0,mistakes=0,guide=true,run=false,recorded=false,sawModel=false,playing=false;frame(`${nav('PIANO MELODY',i,SONGS.length)}<span class="v59-tag">MELODY ${i+1} · ${x.bpm} BPM</span><h1>${E(x.title)}</h1><div class="v59-box"><p id="v60PianoPrompt"><b>Do this:</b> Press the green piano key. When it sounds, press the next one. You'll make a little melody.</p><p class="v59-count" id="v59Note">Note <strong>1</strong> of ${x.notes.length}</p><div class="v59-keyboard" id="v59Keyboard" role="group" aria-label="Playable one-octave piano keyboard">${keys.map(k=>`<button type="button" class="v59-white" data-note="${k}" aria-label="Play ${k}"><span>${k.replace('4','').replace('5','⁵')}</span></button>`).join('')}${black.map(([k,pos])=>`<button type="button" tabindex="0" class="v59-black" style="left:${pos}%" data-note="${k}" aria-label="Play black key ${k}"></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v59-mini" id="v60PianoTip">C is immediately left of the pair of black keys. Start there. Green = next key; any key makes a sound.</p><div class="v59-actions"><button id="v59Listen">▶ Hear the whole melody</button><button id="v59Hide">Try with no note labels or highlights</button><button id="v59Restart">Start the melody again</button></div><p id="v59MusicStatus" class="v59-feedback" role="status">Press the highlighted key to begin. A wrong note is just a chance to find it again.</p><div id="v59After" class="v59-teach" hidden><b>You played the phrase.</b><p>${E(x.why)}</p><p class="v59-mini" id="v59PianoLevel"></p><button class="v59-btn primary" id="v59Replay">Play again from memory</button></div></div><div class="v59-actions"><button id="v59Prev">← Previous</button><button id="v59Next" class="v59-btn primary">Next melody →</button><button id="v59Foundation">Piano from the beginning</button></div>`);wire();const fb=document.getElementById('v59MusicStatus'),nstat=document.getElementById('v59Note'),buttons=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#v59Keyboard [data-note]'));
 function paint(){document.getElementById('v59Keyboard').classList.toggle('v60-keys-unlabeled',!guide);document.getElementById('v60PianoPrompt').innerHTML=guide?'<b>Do this:</b> Press the green key, then the next green key to play a melody.':'<b>Memory challenge:</b> The labels and highlights are hidden. Try the notes in the same order from memory.';document.getElementById('v60PianoTip').textContent=guide?'C is immediately left of the pair of black keys. Green = next note; any key makes a sound.':'If stuck, choose Show labels and green highlights to practice with guidance.';buttons.forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('target',guide&&x.notes[notesDone]===b.dataset.note));nstat.innerHTML=`Note <strong>${Math.min(notesDone+1,x.notes.length)}</strong> of ${x.notes.length}`}
 function reset(){notesDone=0;mistakes=0;recorded=false;run=false;playing=false;document.getElementById('v59After').hidden=true;fb.textContent=guide?'Start on the green key.':'The note labels and highlights are hidden. Play the phrase from memory; tap Show green highlights if needed.';paint()}
 function input(note){if(playing)return;const b=buttons.find(b=>b.dataset.note===note);if(b){b.classList.add('pressed');later(()=>b.classList.remove('pressed'),175)}tone(note);if(notesDone>=x.notes.length)return;const wanted=x.notes[notesDone];if(note!==wanted){mistakes++;fb.textContent=guide?'Not quite. Find the green '+wanted.replace('4','').replace('5',' high')+' key.':'Different note. Try again; turn the guide on if you need it.';return}notesDone++;if(notesDone<x.notes.length){fb.textContent='Good. Next note.';paint();return}paint();if(!recorded){mark('melody:'+i,guide||mistakes>0?'assisted':'independent');recorded=true}const d=document.getElementById('v59After');d.hidden=false;document.getElementById('v59PianoLevel').textContent=guide||mistakes>0?'You completed this with a visual guide or retries. Try without highlights next.':'You played every note from memory on your first attempt.';fb.textContent='That was a real melody. Hear how the last note feels like an ending?'}
 buttons.forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>input(b.dataset.note));paint();document.getElementById('v59Listen').onclick=()=>{if(playing)return;playing=true;sawModel=true;fb.textContent='Listen to the phrase. Each note lasts one beat; then try it yourself.';const c=audio(),beat=60/x.bpm; x.notes.forEach((note,j)=>{if(c)tone(note,c.currentTime+.12+j*beat,Math.min(beat*.7,.5));later(()=>{const key=buttons.find(b=>b.dataset.note===note);key?.classList.add('pressed');later(()=>key?.classList.remove('pressed'),170)},120+j*beat*1000)});later(()=>{playing=false;fb.textContent='Your turn. Start on the green key.'},250+x.notes.length*beat*1000)};document.getElementById('v59Hide').onclick=()=>{guide=!guide;document.getElementById('v59Hide').textContent=guide?'Try with no note labels or highlights':'Show labels and green highlights';reset()};document.getElementById('v59Restart').onclick=reset;document.getElementById('v59Replay').onclick=()=>{guide=false;document.getElementById('v59Hide').textContent='Show labels and green highlights';reset()};document.getElementById('v59Prev').onclick=()=>piano(i-1);document.getElementById('v59Next').onclick=()=>piano(Math.min(SONGS.length-1,i+1));document.getElementById('v59Foundation').onclick=()=>v35Open('piano',0);
}
function medicine(i){i=Number.isInteger(i)?Math.max(0,Math.min(CASES.length-1,i)):next(CASES,'case');const x=CASES[i];let step=0,missed=false,help=false,finished=false,chosen=false;function draw(){const q=x.steps[step],order=[0,1,2];let mix=((i+1)*123457)^((step+1)*104729)^q.q.length;for(let k=2;k>0;k--){mix^=mix<<13;mix^=mix>>>17;mix^=mix<<5;const j=(mix>>>0)%(k+1);[order[k],order[j]]=[order[j],order[k]]};frame(`${nav('CLINICAL REASONING',i,CASES.length)}<span class="v59-tag">CASE ${i+1} · QUESTION ${step+1}/3</span><h1>${E(x.title)}</h1><div class="v59-box"><p class="v59-brief"><b>Patient story:</b> ${E(x.scene)}</p><h2 style="font:700 22px Georgia,serif;margin:18px 0 8px">${E(q.q)}</h2><div class="v59-choices">${order.map(j=>`<button type="button" data-choice="${j}">${E(q.options[j])}</button>`).join('')}</div><p id="v59Feedback" class="v59-feedback" role="status">Pick the most useful answer. We'll explain the mechanism.</p><div class="v59-actions"><button id="v59Help">Smaller hint</button><button id="v59Show">Show reasoning</button></div><div id="v59Reason" class="v59-teach" hidden><b>Why this is the answer</b><p>${E(q.explain)}</p></div><div class="v59-actions"><button id="v59StepNext" class="v59-btn primary" hidden>${step===2?'Finish case':'Next decision →'}</button></div></div><div class="v59-actions"><button id="v59Prev">← Previous case</button><button id="v59Next">Next case →</button><button id="v59Foundation">Medical school foundations</button></div><p class="v59-mini">Educational cases only. Not a tool for patient diagnosis or treatment.</p>`);wire();const feedback=document.getElementById('v59Feedback'),reason=document.getElementById('v59Reason'),nextButton=document.getElementById('v59StepNext');function finish(){chosen=true;reason.hidden=false;nextButton.hidden=false;document.querySelectorAll('[data-choice]').forEach(b=>b.disabled=true);if(step===2&&!finished){mark('case:'+i,missed||help?'assisted':'independent');finished=true}}document.querySelectorAll('[data-choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(chosen)return;const v=Number(b.dataset.choice);if(v===q.correct){b.classList.add('right');feedback.textContent='Yes. '+q.explain;finish()}else{missed=true;b.classList.add('wrong');b.disabled=true;feedback.textContent='Not quite: '+q.options[v]+'. '+q.hint+' Try a different explanation.'}});document.getElementById('v59Help').onclick=()=>{help=true;feedback.textContent=q.hint};document.getElementById('v59Show').onclick=()=>{help=true;const b=document.querySelector(`[data-choice="${q.correct}"]`);b?.classList.add('right');feedback.textContent='The key idea: '+q.explain;finish()};nextButton.onclick=()=>{if(step<2){step++;chosen=false;draw()}else{feedback.textContent='Case completed. You followed the reasoning through all three decisions.';nextButton.disabled=true}};document.getElementById('v59Prev').onclick=()=>medicine(i-1);document.getElementById('v59Next').onclick=()=>medicine(Math.min(CASES.length-1,i+1));document.getElementById('v59Foundation').onclick=()=>v35Open('medicine',0)}draw()}

const topics=[...new Set(missions.map(m=>m.subject))];const iconFor=s=>missions.find(m=>m.subject===s)?.icon||'✦';
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const empty=()=>({items:{},recent:null});function load(){try{const o=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE)||'null');return o&&typeof o==='object'&&!Array.isArray(o)?{items:o.items&&typeof o.items==='object'&&!Array.isArray(o.items)?o.items:{},recent:o.recent||null}:empty()}catch{return empty()}}
function save(o){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(o))}catch{}}
let active=null,priorFocus=null,focusTrap=null;
function ensureUI(){if(document.querySelector('#v62Overlay'))return;let e=document.createElement('div');e.id='v62Overlay';e.innerHTML='<section class="v62-shell" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Study Table discovery" tabindex="-1"><header class="v62-top"><div class="v62-mark"><span class="v62-star" aria-hidden="true">✳</span> STUDY TABLE <span class="v62-subbrand">/ a small discovery</span></div><button id="v62Close" class="v62-x" type="button" aria-label="Close discovery">✕</button></header><div id="v62Body" aria-live="polite"></div></section>';document.body.appendChild(e);e.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(event.target===e)close()});e.querySelector('#v62Close').onclick=close;
 document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(!e.classList.contains('open'))return;if(event.key==='Escape'){close();return}if(event.key!=='Tab')return;let nodes=[...e.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),input:not([disabled])')].filter(el=>el.offsetParent!==null);if(!nodes.length)return;const first=nodes[0],last=nodes[nodes.length-1];if(event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){event.preventDefault();last.focus()}else if(!event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){event.preventDefault();first.focus()}});
}
function open(subject){ensureUI();priorFocus=document.activeElement;document.querySelector('#v62Overlay').classList.add('open');document.body.classList.add('v62-freeze');if(subject)chooseSubject(subject);else chooseMenu();document.querySelector('.v62-shell').focus()}
function close(){let e=document.querySelector('#v62Overlay');if(!e||!e.classList.contains('open'))return;e.classList.remove('open');document.body.classList.remove('v62-freeze');active=null;if(priorFocus&&priorFocus.isConnected)priorFocus.focus()}
function body(html){document.querySelector('#v62Body').innerHTML=html;const shell=document.querySelector('.v62-shell');if(shell)shell.scrollTop=0}
function countDone(subject){const rows=load().items;return missions.filter(m=>(!subject||m.subject===subject)&&rows[m.id]?.count>0).length}
function pick(subject){const rows=load().items;const eligible=missions.filter(m=>!subject||m.subject===subject),untried=eligible.filter(m=>!rows[m.id]?.count);if(untried.length)return untried[0];return eligible.slice().sort((a,b)=>(rows[a.id]?.last||0)-(rows[b.id]?.last||0))[0]}
function chooseMenu(){active=null;const d=load(),last=missions.find(m=>m.id===d.recent);body(`<div class="v62-menu"><p class="v62-eyebrow">NO SCHEDULE · NO STREAKS · START ANYWHERE</p><h1>Find something <em>surprisingly interesting.</em></h1><p class="v62-lede">A little story. One idea that makes sense. Two decisions to see if it sticks. You can see the reasoning whenever you want.</p><div class="v62-prime"><div><span class="v62-tag">YOUR NEXT FOUR MINUTES</span><h2>${esc(last?'Something new, or pick up where you left off.':'An interesting idea is waiting.')}</h2><p>You don’t have to pick a whole course. Just try one small discovery.</p></div><button id="v62Surprise" class="v62-btn primary">Pick for me <span>↗</span></button></div>${last?`<button id="v62Resume" class="v62-resume">↻ Try ${esc(last.title)} again <span>→</span></button>`:''}<div class="v62-pick-title"><h2>Or choose what sounds good</h2><small>${countDone()} of ${missions.length} discoveries explored</small></div><div class="v62-grid">${topics.map(s=>`<button class="v62-topic" data-subject="${esc(s)}"><span class="v62-icon" aria-hidden="true">${esc(iconFor(s))}</span><strong>${esc(s)}</strong><small>${countDone(s)} / ${missions.filter(m=>m.subject===s).length} explored</small><span class="v62-arrow">↗</span></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v62-tail">Prefer the full curriculum? Close this window and everything is right where you left it.</p></div>`);
 document.getElementById('v62Surprise').onclick=()=>{const untried=missions.filter(m=>!load().items[m.id]?.count);const arr=untried.length?untried:missions;startMission(arr[Math.floor(Math.random()*arr.length)].id)};
 document.getElementById('v62Resume')?.addEventListener('click',()=>startMission(last.id));document.querySelectorAll('[data-subject]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>chooseSubject(b.dataset.subject));}
function chooseSubject(subject){const list=missions.filter(m=>m.subject===subject);if(!list.length){chooseMenu();return}body(`<div class="v62-section"><button class="v62-link" id="v62Back">← All subjects</button><p class="v62-eyebrow">${esc(subject.toUpperCase())} · SMALL DISCOVERIES</p><h1>Which question catches you?</h1><p class="v62-lede">Try whichever sounds interesting. You don't have to go in order.</p><div class="v62-missions">${list.map(m=>`<button data-mission="${esc(m.id)}" class="v62-mission"><span class="v62-mission-icon">${esc(m.icon)}</span><div><strong>${esc(m.title)}</strong><small>${esc(m.tag)}${load().items[m.id]?.count?' · Explored':''}</small></div><span>↗</span></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v62-note">Want the bigger course? Close the window to return to all lessons.</p></div>`);document.getElementById('v62Back').onclick=chooseMenu;document.querySelectorAll('[data-mission]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>startMission(b.dataset.mission));}
function startMission(id){ensureUI();const m=missions.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!m)return chooseMenu();active={m,step:0,wrong:false,helped:false,attempts:0,answered:[false,false],result:['',''],recorded:false};renderIntro()}
function crumb(){const x=active.m;return `<nav class="v62-crumb"><button id="v62All" class="v62-link" type="button">← Explore</button><span>${esc(x.subject)} <span aria-hidden="true">·</span> ${active.step===0?'The idea':active.step===1?'First try':active.step===2?'A new situation':'What you learned'}</span></nav>`}
function wireBack(){document.getElementById('v62All').onclick=chooseMenu}
function renderIntro(){active.step=0;const x=active.m;body(`<div class="v62-play">${crumb()}<div class="v62-progress"><i class="on"></i><i></i><i></i></div><div class="v62-stage"><span class="v62-eyebrow">${esc(x.tag)}</span><h1>${esc(x.title)}</h1><div class="v62-story"><span class="v62-story-label">PICTURE THIS</span><p>${esc(x.hook)}</p></div><h2>Here’s the idea</h2><p>${esc(x.idea)}</p><div class="v62-example"><span>MAKE IT CONCRETE</span><p>${esc(x.example)}</p></div><div class="v62-action"><button class="v62-btn primary" id="v62Try">Okay, let me try it <span>→</span></button><small>It’s fine to get it wrong. The explanations are the point.</small></div></div></div>`);wireBack();document.getElementById('v62Try').onclick=()=>renderQuestion(0)}
function renderQuestion(index){active.step=index+1;let x=active.m,q=x.questions[index],already=active.answered[index];body(`<div class="v62-play">${crumb()}<div class="v62-progress"><i class="on"></i><i class="${index>=0?'on':''}"></i><i class="${index>=1?'on':''}"></i></div><div class="v62-stage"><span class="v62-eyebrow">${index===0?'LET’S TEST THE IDEA':'SAME IDEA, DIFFERENT SITUATION'}</span><h1>${esc(q.ask)}</h1><p class="v62-lede">Pick the answer that makes sense to you. You can always see a smaller step.</p><div class="v62-choices">${q.options.map((option,k)=>`<button class="v62-choice" data-answer="${k}" type="button"><span class="v62-choice-letter">${String.fromCharCode(65+k)}</span><span>${esc(option)}</span><span class="v62-choice-arrow">↗</span></button>`).join('')}</div><div id="v62Feedback" class="v62-feedback" role="status" hidden></div><div class="v62-smallhelp"><button id="v62Hint" class="v62-link" type="button">Break it down</button><button id="v62Reveal" class="v62-link" type="button">Show answer & reasoning</button></div><div class="v62-action" id="v62NextWrap" hidden><button class="v62-btn primary" id="v62Next">${index===0?'Try it in a new situation':'See what you learned'} <span>→</span></button></div></div></div>`);
 wireBack();let area=document.getElementById('v62Feedback');function show(text,kind){area.hidden=false;area.className='v62-feedback '+kind;area.innerHTML=text}
 function solved(which,assisted){if(active.answered[index])return;active.answered[index]=true;active.result[index]=assisted?'assisted':'first-try';document.querySelectorAll('[data-answer]').forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;b.classList.toggle('correct',+b.dataset.answer===q.correct);});document.querySelector('#v62NextWrap').hidden=false;document.getElementById('v62Hint').hidden=true;document.getElementById('v62Reveal').hidden=true;if(assisted)active.helped=true;show(`<strong>${assisted?'Here’s the reasoning':'That’s it.'}</strong><p>${esc(q.feedback[q.correct])}</p>`,assisted?'learning':'right');document.getElementById('v62Next').onclick=()=>index===0?renderQuestion(1):finishMission();}
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-answer]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const a=Number(b.dataset.answer);if(active.answered[index])return;active.attempts++;if(a===q.correct){solved(a,active.wrong||active.helped)}else{active.wrong=true;b.disabled=true;b.classList.add('wrong');show(`<strong>Not quite. Here’s what’s different.</strong><p>${esc(q.feedback[a])}</p><p class="v62-try-again">Try another answer—there’s no penalty.</p>`,'try')}});
 document.getElementById('v62Hint').onclick=()=>{active.helped=true;show(`<strong>Break it down</strong><p>${esc(q.hint)}</p>`,'learning')};document.getElementById('v62Reveal').onclick=()=>{solved(q.correct,true)};
}
function finishMission(){active.step=3;const x=active.m,independent=active.result.every(v=>v==='first-try');const data=load(),prev=data.items[x.id]||{count:0,independent:0,assisted:0},entry={count:Math.min(999,(prev.count||0)+1),independent:(prev.independent||0)+(independent?1:0),assisted:(prev.assisted||0)+(independent?0:1),last:Date.now(),lastResult:independent?'independent':'with-help'};data.items[x.id]=entry;data.recent=x.id;save(data);active.recorded=true;

const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const fresh=()=>({version:1,items:{},recent:null});
function read(){try{let o=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE)||'null');if(!o||typeof o!=='object'||Array.isArray(o))return fresh();return {version:1,items:o.items&&typeof o.items==='object'&&!Array.isArray(o.items)?o.items:{},recent:typeof o.recent==='string'?o.recent:null}}catch{return fresh()}}
function write(d){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(d))}catch{}}
const MS_HOUR=3600000, DAYS=[1,3,7,21,45];
function seed(id){if(!BANK[id])return;const d=read(),source=OLD.load().items[id];if(!source?.count)return;const row=d.items[id]||{};if(!Number.isFinite(row.next)){row.next=Number(source.last||Date.now())+12*MS_HOUR;row.round=0;row.attempts=0;row.variant=0;row.first=null;d.items[id]=row;write(d);}}
function sync(){for(const m of OLD.missions)seed(m.id)}
function eligible(){sync();const data=read();return OLD.missions.filter(m=>data.items[m.id]&&Number.isFinite(data.items[m.id].next)).map(m=>({m,row:data.items[m.id]})).sort((a,b)=>(a.row.next-b.row.next));}
function due(){return eligible().filter(x=>x.row.next<=Date.now())}
let current=null,priorFocus=null;
function ensure(){if(document.getElementById('v63Layer'))return;const root=document.createElement('div');root.id='v63Layer';root.innerHTML='<section class="v63-panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="A fresh challenge" tabindex="-1"><div class="v63-top"><span>STUDY TABLE <small>/ another angle</small></span><button type="button" id="v63Close" aria-label="Close challenge">✕</button></div><div id="v63Inner"></div></section>';document.body.appendChild(root);root.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===root)close()});root.querySelector('#v63Close').onclick=close;root.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();close()}if(e.key!=='Tab')return;const buttons=[...root.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled])')].filter(el=>el.getClientRects().length);if(!buttons.length)return;const first=buttons[0],last=buttons[buttons.length-1];if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){e.preventDefault();last.focus()}else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){e.preventDefault();first.focus()}})}
function render(html){document.getElementById('v63Inner').innerHTML=html;const panel=document.querySelector('.v63-panel');panel.scrollTop=0;panel.focus()}
function open(id){sync();const arr=eligible();const item=(id?arr.find(x=>x.m.id===id):due()[0]||arr[0]);if(!item){OLD.open();return}ensure();priorFocus=document.activeElement;document.getElementById('v63Layer').classList.add('open');document.body.classList.add('v63-freeze');begin(item.m.id)}
function close(){const root=document.getElementById('v63Layer');if(!root||!root.classList.contains('open'))return;root.classList.remove('open');document.body.classList.remove('v63-freeze');current=null;if(priorFocus?.isConnected&&priorFocus.getClientRects().length)priorFocus.focus();else{const home=document.getElementById('v62HomeStart')||document.getElementById('v35Quick');if(home?.getClientRects().length)home.focus()}}
function begin(id){const row=read().items[id]||{variant:0};const card=BANK[id]?.[row.variant%2],m=OLD.missions.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!card||!m)return close();current={id,m,card,first:true,help:false,wrong:false,answered:false};const order=[0,1,2];for(let i=order.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[order[i],order[j]]=[order[j],order[i]]}const isDue=(row.next||0)<=Date.now();render(`<div class="v63-stage"><p class="v63-kicker">${esc(m.subject)} · ${isDue?'WELCOME BACK':'A FRESH ANGLE'}</p><h1>${esc(m.title)}</h1><p class="v63-lead">${isDue?'You met this idea before. Can you still apply it in a different situation?':'A new situation to check whether the idea actually transfers.'}</p><div class="v63-prompt"><small>TRY IT WITHOUT A HINT FIRST</small><h2>${esc(card.ask)}</h2></div><div class="v63-options">${order.map((n,i)=>`<button class="v63-option" type="button" data-v63-answer="${n}"><span>${String.fromCharCode(65+i)}</span>${esc(card.opts[n])}</button>`).join('')}</div><div role="status" class="v63-feedback" id="v63Feedback" hidden></div><div class="v63-helpers"><button id="v63Hint" type="button">Make it simpler</button><button id="v63Show" type="button">Show the reasoning</button></div><div class="v63-actions" id="v63NextWrap" hidden><button class="v63-primary" type="button" id="v63Next">See where this connects →</button></div><p class="v63-footnote">Getting help is part of learning. It just won’t be counted as practice without help.</p></div>`);
 const show=(html,cls='')=>{let el=document.getElementById('v63Feedback');el.hidden=false;el.className='v63-feedback '+cls;el.innerHTML=html};
 function complete(assisted){if(current.answered)return;current.answered=true;const d=read(),r=d.items[id]||{round:0,attempts:0,variant:0};r.attempts=(r.attempts||0)+1;r.last=Date.now();r.first=!assisted;r.round=assisted?0:Math.min(5,(r.round||0)+1);r.variant=((r.variant||0)+1)%2;r.next=Date.now()+(assisted?4*MS_HOUR:DAYS[Math.min(r.round-1,DAYS.length-1)]*24*MS_HOUR);d.items[id]=r;d.recent=id;write(d);document.querySelectorAll('[data-v63-answer]').forEach(el=>{el.disabled=true;el.classList.toggle('right',+el.dataset.v63Answer===card.correct)});document.getElementById('v63Hint').hidden=true;document.getElementById('v63Show').hidden=true;document.getElementById('v63NextWrap').hidden=false;show(`<strong>${assisted?'Now you’ve seen how it works.':'You applied it without a hint.'}</strong><p>${esc(card.why[card.correct])}</p><p class="v63-spacing">${assisted?'We’ll offer another check sooner, with a different example.':'We’ll bring this back later to see if it sticks.'}</p>`,'solved')}
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v63-answer]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(current.answered)return;let n=+b.dataset.v63Answer;if(n===card.correct)complete(current.help||current.wrong);else{current.wrong=true;b.disabled=true;b.classList.add('wrong');show(`<strong>Almost. Here’s the distinction.</strong><p>${esc(card.why[n])}</p><small>Try one of the other choices.</small>`,'miss')}});
 document.getElementById('v63Hint').onclick=()=>{current.help=true;show(`<strong>One smaller step</strong><p>${esc(card.hint)}</p>`,'hint')};document.getElementById('v63Show').onclick=()=>{current.help=true;complete(true)};document.getElementById('v63Next').onclick=connection;
 }

function empty(){return{version:64,items:{},drafts:{},recent:null}}
function read(){try{const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY));return d&&typeof d==='object'&&!Array.isArray(d)?{...empty(),...d,items:d.items&&typeof d.items==='object'?d.items:{},drafts:d.drafts&&typeof d.drafts==='object'?d.drafts:{}}:empty()}catch{return empty()}}

function menu(){const completed=new Set(completedIDs()),rows=PREV.missions.map(m=>({m,ready:completed.has(m.id)}));render(`<article class="v64-stage"><span class="v64-kicker">YOUR WORDS, NOT A MULTIPLE-CHOICE ANSWER</span><h1>What could you explain to a friend?</h1><p class="v64-lead">Choose any idea. function begin(id){const card=BANK[id],mission=PREV.missions.find(m=>m.id===id);if(!card||!mission)return menu();const data=read(),entry=data.items[id]||{},prior=(PREV.load().items[id]?.last)||0;const justReviewed=REV.read().items[id]?.last||0;const latest=Math.max(prior,justReviewed,entry.last||0);const cold=prior>0&&Date.now()-latest>=12*3600000;state={id,card,mission,cold,hint:false,seen:false,typed:false,answer:String(data.drafts[id]||''),follow:''};drawPrompt()}
function saveDraft(value){if(!state)return;state.answer=String(value).slice(0,2000);const d=read();d.drafts[state.id]=state.answer;write(d)}
function drawPrompt(){const s=state,c=s.card,m=s.mission;render(`<article class="v64-stage"><div class="v64-mini-nav"><button id="v64Menu" type="button">← All ideas</button><span>${esc(m.subject)} · ${esc(m.title)}</span></div><span class="v64-kicker">${s.cold?'A RETURN VISIT — TEST YOUR RECALL':'A LOW-PRESSURE EXPLANATION'}</span><h1>Say it in your own words.</h1><p class="v64-lead">Imagine a friend who hasn't studied this subject. Don't aim for perfect prose. Help them understand.</p><div class="v64-question"><span>EXPLAIN THIS</span><h2>${esc(c.prompt)}</h2></div><label for="v64Answer" class="v64-label">Your explanation <small>Saved only in this browser</small></label><textarea id="v64Answer" rows="5" maxlength="2000" spellcheck="true" placeholder="Start with the part you're sure about…">${esc(s.answer)}</textarea><div class="v64-count"><span id="v64WordCount">${s.answer.trim()?s.answer.trim().split(/\s+/).length:0} words</span><span>No automated grading</span></div><div id="v64MicroHelp" class="v64-micro" hidden></div><div class="v64-toolbar"><button id="v64Small" type="button">Make the question smaller</button><button id="v64Starter" type="button">Give me a first sentence</button></div><div class="v64-primary-row"><button id="v64Compare" type="button" class="v64-primary">Compare with a clear explanation →</button><button id="v64Show" type="button" class="v64-light">Just teach me the answer</button></div><p class="v64-subtle">Help is always available. function comparison(){const s=state,c=s.card,wasWritten=s.answer.trim().length>=30&&!s.hint&&!s.seen;state.eligibleForSelfReport=wasWritten&&s.cold;state.seen=true;render(`<article class="v64-stage"><div class="v64-mini-nav"><button id="v64Back" type="button">← Revise my explanation</button><span>${esc(s.mission.subject)} · compare</span></div><span class="v64-kicker">SEE WHAT YOU UNDERSTOOD — AND WHAT'S MISSING</span><h1>Compare the reasoning.</h1><p class="v64-lead">Compare what you wrote with the worked explanation. Which ideas did you include?</p>${s.answer.trim()?`<div class="v64-own"><span>YOUR WORDS</span><p>${esc(s.answer)}</p></div>`:'<div class="v64-own"><span>STARTING FROM AN EXAMPLE</span><p>You chose to see the explanation first. That is still useful practice.</p></div>'}<div class="v64-model"><span>A CLEAR EXPLANATION</span><p>${esc(c.model)}</p></div><div class="v64-pitfall"><strong>A common trap</strong><p>${esc(c.pitfall)}</p></div><fieldset class="v64-checks"><legend>What did your original explanation include?</legend>${c.criteria.map(([a,b],i)=>`<label><input type="checkbox" data-v64-check="${i}"/><span><strong>${esc(a)}</strong><small>${esc(b)}</small></span></label>`).join('')}</fieldset><p class="v64-caveat">These checkboxes are your reflection, not an independently verified grade. Reading the model answer is not the same as remembering it later.</p><div class="v64-primary-row"><button id="v64Push" class="v64-primary" type="button">Try a different situation →</button><button id="v64Save" class="v64-light" type="button">Save and finish</button></div><button id="v64Coach" class="v64-online" type="button">Ask an online tutor about my explanation (optional)</button><div id="v64CoachReply" class="v64-coach-reply" hidden role="status"></div></article>`);document.getElementById('v64Back').onclick=drawPrompt;document.getElementById('v64Push').onclick=()=>{saveReflection();followUp()};document.getElementById('v64Save').onclick=()=>{saveReflection();finish()};document.getElementById('v64Coach').onclick=askCoach}
function saveReflection(){if(!state)return;let s=state,checks=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-v64-check]')].map(x=>x.checked);if(!checks.length)return;const data=read(),p=data.items[s.id]||{attempts:0};p.attempts=(p.attempts||0)+1;p.last=Date.now();p.criteria=checks;p.selfReported=checks.every(Boolean)&&s.eligibleForSelfReport;p.kind=p.selfReported?'self-reported recall':'guided reflection';p.next=Date.now()+(p.selfReported?2*24:12)*3600000;data.items[s.id]=p;data.recent=s.id;data.drafts[s.id]=s.answer;write(data);s.reflected=true;s.selfReported=p.selfReported}
function followUp(){const s=state,c=s.card;render(`<article class="v64-stage"><span class="v64-kicker">NEW SITUATION, SAME IDEA</span><h1>Does your explanation travel?</h1><p class="v64-lead">This isn't the same question with the words changed. Apply the principle to a related situation.</p><div class="v64-question"><span>TRY IT HERE</span><h2>${esc(c.transfer)}</h2></div><label for="v64FollowAnswer" class="v64-label">What would you say?</label><textarea id="v64FollowAnswer" rows="4" maxlength="1400" placeholder="Use the idea. Keep it simple.">${esc(s.follow)}</textarea><div class="v64-primary-row"><button id="v64ShowTransfer" class="v64-primary" type="button">See how to reason through it →</button><button id="v64Stop" type="button" class="v64-light">Enough for now</button></div><div id="v64TransferModel" hidden></div></article>`);document.getElementById('v64FollowAnswer').oninput=e=>{s.follow=e.target.value.slice(0,1400)};document.getElementById('v64Stop').onclick=()=>{const d=read();if(d.items[s.id]){d.items[s.id].transferWrote=s.follow.trim().length>=15;write(d)}finish()};document.getElementById('v64ShowTransfer').onclick=()=>{const el=document.getElementById('v64TransferModel');el.hidden=false;el.innerHTML=`<div class="v64-model"><span>WORKED REASONING</span><p>${esc(c.transferModel)}</p></div><p class="v64-caveat">Compare the principle, not just the wording. Another example after some time has passed is the better memory test.</p><button id="v64Finish" class="v64-primary" type="button">Finish this idea →</button>`;document.getElementById('v64Finish').onclick=finish;document.getElementById('v64ShowTransfer').disabled=true;const d=read();if(d.items[s.id]){d.items[s.id].transferSeen=true;d.items[s.id].transferWrote=s.follow.trim().length>=15;write(d)}}}
function finish(){const s=state;if(!s)return;const entry=read().items[s.id];render(`<article class="v64-stage v64-finished"><span class="v64-kicker">ONE IDEA, BETTER UNDERSTOOD</span><h1>${s.answer.trim().length>=20?'You tried explaining the idea.':'You saw how the idea works.'}</h1><p>${entry?.selfReported?'You reported that you could explain all three parts from memory. We’ll offer another check in a couple of days. This is self-assessed, not externally verified.':'You worked through an explanation and identified what was missing. Another example will be available for review.'}</p><div class="v64-next-choices"><button class="v64-primary" id="v64Another" type="button">Explain another idea →</button><button id="v64Explore" type="button">Explore the full lesson</button><button id="v64Leave" type="button">I'm done for now</button></div></article>`);document.getElementById('v64Another').onclick=menu;document.getElementById('v64Explore').onclick=()=>{const {id}=s;close();PREV.open(PREV.missions.find(m=>m.id===id).subject);PREV.startMission(id)};document.getElementById('v64Leave').onclick=close}
async function askCoach(){const s=state,reply=document.getElementById('v64CoachReply'),btn=document.getElementById('v64Coach');if(!reply||!s)return;reply.hidden=false;if(location.protocol==='file:'){reply.textContent='The optional online tutor needs a hosted, configured server. Your offline worked explanation and checklist are available above.';return}btn.disabled=true;reply.textContent='Asking the configured tutor…';const abort=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>abort.abort(),22000);try{const resp=await fetch('/api/tutor',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},signal:abort.signal,body:JSON.stringify({context:{subject:s.mission.subject,mode:'teachback-feedback',title:s.mission.title,question:s.card.prompt,modelAnswer:s.card.model,explanation:s.card.pitfall,clue:s.card.small},messages:[{role:'user',content:'Review my attempt without pretending there is a single correct wording. Explain one thing I understood, one thing that needs correction or clarification, then ask one brief follow-up question. My explanation:\n'+(s.answer||'[I did not write an explanation]')}]})});const data=await resp.json();reply.textContent=data.reply||data.error||'No feedback was returned.'}catch{reply.textContent='The online tutor is unavailable. The authored explanation above works offline.'}finally{clearTimeout(timer);btn.disabled=false}}
function decorate(){if(locked)return;locked=true;queueMicrotask(()=>{locked=false;try{const box=document.getElementById('v62HomeFeature');if(box){const actions=box.querySelector('.v62-home-buttons');if(actions&&!document.getElementById('v64Home')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v64Home';b.type='button';b.textContent='Explain it in your words →';b.onclick=()=>open(completedIDs().length?undefined:null);actions.append(b)}}const comp=document.querySelector('#v63Layer.open .v63-end .v63-final-actions');if(comp&&!document.getElementById('v64AfterReview')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.id='v64AfterReview';b.textContent='Now explain it without choices →';b.onclick=()=>{const data=REV.read(),id=data.recent;if(id)open(id);else menu()};comp.prepend(b)}const done=document.querySelector('#v62Body .v62-finish-actions');if(done&&!document.getElementById('v64AfterDiscovery')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v64AfterDiscovery';b.type='button';b.textContent='Explain this in my own words →';b.onclick=()=>{const id=PREV.load().recent;PREV.close();open(id)};done.append(b)}}catch(e){console.warn('Explain it yourself entry skipped',e)}})}
function init(){const o=new MutationObserver(decorate);o.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});decorate();const tag=document.querySelector('header .brand small');if(tag)tag.textContent='V64 · LEARN TO EXPLAIN';}
window.studyTableV64={version:64,open,close,begin,menu,read,BANK,completedIDs};
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
})();

</script>
